"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Search, LibraryBig, Star, Pin } from "lucide-react";
import type { ColumnDef } from "@tanstack/react-table";
import {
  useAdminCourseCatalog,
  useAdminCategoryList,
  useToggleCourseFlag,
  type AdminCourse
} from "@saachi/api-client";
import { DataTable, StatusPill, Select } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Draft", value: "draft" },
  { label: "Pending review", value: "pending_review" },
  { label: "Published", value: "published" },
  { label: "Rejected", value: "rejected" },
  { label: "Archived", value: "archived" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<AdminCourse["status"], "success" | "warning" | "error" | "info" | "neutral"> = {
  draft: "neutral",
  pending_review: "warning",
  published: "success",
  rejected: "error",
  archived: "info"
};

export default function CourseCatalogPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [categoryId, setCategoryId] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");

  const { data: categories } = useAdminCategoryList();
  const { data, isLoading } = useAdminCourseCatalog({
    search: search || undefined,
    categoryId: categoryId || undefined,
    status: status || undefined
  });
  const toggleFlag = useToggleCourseFlag();

  const categoryOptions = useMemo(
    () => (categories ?? []).map((c) => ({ label: c.name, value: c.id })),
    [categories]
  );

  const columns = useMemo<ColumnDef<AdminCourse, any>[]>(
    () => [
      {
        header: "Course",
        accessorKey: "title",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="h-9 w-16 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-control border border-line bg-paper-dim">
              {row.original.thumbnailUrl && (
                // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                <img src={row.original.thumbnailUrl} alt={row.original.title} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="max-w-[260px]">
              <div className="truncate font-medium text-ink">{row.original.title}</div>
              <div className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{row.original.instructorName}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Category",
        accessorKey: "categoryId",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{getValue()}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Price",
        accessorKey: "price",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-ink">
            {formatCurrency(row.original.price, row.original.currency)}
          </span>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Status",
        accessorKey: "status",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => {
          const value = getValue() as AdminCourse["status"];
          return <StatusPill label={value.replace("_", " ")} tone={STATUS_TONE[value]} />;
        }
      },
      {
        header: "Enrollments",
        accessorKey: "enrollmentCount",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="font-mono text-ink-soft">{(getValue() as number).toLocaleString()}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Submitted",
        accessorKey: "submittedAt",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatDate(getValue() as string)}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Curate",
        id: "curate",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                toggleFlag.mutate({ id: row.original.id, field: "featured" });
              }}
              title="Toggle featured"
              className={`flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-control border transition-colors duration-150 ease-out ${
                row.original.featured ? "border-ink bg-ink text-paper" : "border-line text-ink-soft hover:text-ink"
              }`}
            >
              <Star size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                toggleFlag.mutate({ id: row.original.id, field: "pinned" });
              }}
              title="Toggle pinned"
              className={`flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-control border transition-colors duration-150 ease-out ${
                row.original.pinned ? "border-ink bg-ink text-paper" : "border-line text-ink-soft hover:text-ink"
              }`}
            >
              <Pin size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            </button>
          </div>
        )
      }
    ],
    [toggleFlag]
  );

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1200px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
          <LibraryBig size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Course Catalog</h1>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Full catalog across every status - feature or pin courses for curation.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <DataTable
        columns={columns}
        data={data ?? []}
        isLoading={isLoading}
        emptyTitle="No courses found"
        emptyDescription="Try adjusting your search or filters."
        toolbar={
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft" />
              <input
                placeholder="Search by title or instructor"
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                className="h-10 w-full rounded-control border border-line bg-card pl-9 pr-3 text-[13px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:border-line-strong"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[220px]">
              <Select placeholder="All categories" options={categoryOptions} value={categoryId} onChange={(e) => setCategoryId(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
              <Select placeholder="All statuses" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
          </div>
        }
      />
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\courses\categories\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Tags, Plus, Pencil, Trash2, X } from "lucide-react";
import {
  useAdminCategoryList,
  useCreateCategory,
  useUpdateCategory,
  useDeleteCategory,
  type AdminCategory
} from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Input, Modal, ShimmerTableRow, Badge } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function CategoryManagementPage() {
  const { data: categories, isLoading } = useAdminCategoryList();
  const createCategory = useCreateCategory();
  const updateCategory = useUpdateCategory();
  const deleteCategory = useDeleteCategory();

  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<AdminCategory | null>(null);
  const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [tagInput, setTagInput] = useState("");
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<AdminCategory | null>(null);

  const openCreate = () => {
    setName("");
    setTags([]);
    setTagInput("");
    setShowCreate(true);
  };

  const openEdit = (category: AdminCategory) => {
    setEditing(category);
    setName(category.name);
    setTags(category.tags);
    setTagInput("");
  };

  const addTag = () => {
    const value = tagInput.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!value || tags.includes(value)) return;
    setTags((prev) => [...prev, value]);
    setTagInput("");
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    if (!name.trim()) return;
    if (editing) {
      updateCategory.mutate({ id: editing.id, name: name.trim(), tags });
      setEditing(null);
    } else {
      createCategory.mutate({ name: name.trim(), tags });
      setShowCreate(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = () => {
    if (!deleteTarget) return;
    deleteCategory.mutate(deleteTarget.id);
    setDeleteTarget(null);
  };

  const modalOpen = showCreate || Boolean(editing);

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[900px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <Tags size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Category & Tag Management</h1>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Taxonomy used across the marketplace and course builder.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button size="sm" onClick={openCreate}>
          <Plus size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> New category
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
        {isLoading ? (
          Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={3} />)
        ) : (
          categories?.map((category) => (
            <div key={category.id} className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-line px-5 py-4 last:border-0 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{category.name}</p>
                  <span className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">/{category.slug}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                  {category.tags.map((tag) => (
                    <Badge key={tag}>{tag}</Badge>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-4">
                <span className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{category.courseCount} courses</span>
                <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => openEdit(category)}
                    className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                  >
                    <Pencil size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(category)}
                    className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error"
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>

      <Modal
        open={modalOpen}
        onClose={() => {
          setShowCreate(false);
          setEditing(null);
        }}
        title={editing ? "Edit category" : "New category"}
        footer={
          <>
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              onClick={() => {
                setShowCreate(false);
                setEditing(null);
              }}
            >
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={handleSave} disabled={!name.trim() || createCategory.isPending || updateCategory.isPending}>
              {editing ? "Save changes" : "Create category"}
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Category name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. AI for Careers & Job Search" />
          <div>
            <label className="mb-1.5 block text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">Tags</label>
            <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
              {tags.map((tag) => (
                <span key={tag} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-line-strong px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] text-ink">
                  {tag}
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => setTags((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t !== tag))} className="text-ink-soft hover:text-error">
                    <X size={11} strokeWidth={2} />
                  </button>
                </span>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <Input
                placeholder="Add a tag and press Enter"
                value={tagInput}
                onChange={(e) => setTagInput(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === "Enter") {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    addTag();
                  }
                }}
                className="h-10 flex-1"
              />
              <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={addTag}>
                Add
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Modal>

      <Modal
        open={Boolean(deleteTarget)}
        onClose={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}
        title="Delete category?"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button variant="danger" onClick={handleDelete} disabled={deleteCategory.isPending}>
              Delete
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <p className="text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
          This will remove <span className="font-semibold text-ink">{deleteTarget?.name}</span> from the taxonomy.
          Courses already assigned to it will need to be recategorized.
        </p>
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\courses\moderation\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { CheckCircle2, XCircle, ClipboardCheck, User, Tag } from "lucide-react";
import { useAdminModerationQueue, useModerateCourse, type AdminCourse } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Textarea, Modal, EmptyState, ShimmerKpiCard } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

export default function CourseModerationQueuePage() {
  const { data: queue, isLoading } = useAdminModerationQueue();
  const moderate = useModerateCourse();
  const [rejectTarget, setRejectTarget] = useState<AdminCourse | null>(null);
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("");

  const handleApprove = (course: AdminCourse) => {
    moderate.mutate({ id: course.id, decision: "published" });
  };

  const handleRejectSubmit = () => {
    if (!rejectTarget) return;
    moderate.mutate({ id: rejectTarget.id, decision: "rejected", reason });
    setRejectTarget(null);
    setReason("");
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1100px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
          <ClipboardCheck size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Course Moderation Queue</h1>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Review newly submitted courses before they go live.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerKpiCard key={i} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : !queue || queue.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<ClipboardCheck size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />}
          title="Queue is clear"
          description="No courses are currently pending review."
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {queue.map((course) => (
            <Card key={course.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
              <div className="h-[90px] w-[160px] shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-control border border-line bg-paper-dim">
                {course.thumbnailUrl && (
                  // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                  <img src={course.thumbnailUrl} alt={course.title} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="flex-1">
                <h3 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{course.title}</h3>
                <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <User size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> {course.instructorName}
                  </span>
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Tag size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> {course.categoryId}
                  </span>
                  <span>Submitted {formatDate(course.submittedAt)}</span>
                  <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-ink">
                    {formatCurrency(course.price, course.currency)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex shrink-0 gap-2">
                <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => setRejectTarget(course)}>
                  <XCircle size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Reject
                </Button>
                <Button size="sm" onClick={() => handleApprove(course)} disabled={moderate.isPending}>
                  <CheckCircle2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Approve
                </Button>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <Modal
        open={Boolean(rejectTarget)}
        onClose={() => setRejectTarget(null)}
        title="Reject course submission"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setRejectTarget(null)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button variant="danger" onClick={handleRejectSubmit} disabled={!reason.trim() || moderate.isPending}>
              {moderate.isPending ? "Sending..." : "Send feedback & reject"}
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <p className="mb-4 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
          Rejecting <span className="font-semibold text-ink">{rejectTarget?.title}</span>. This feedback is sent to
          the instructor so they can revise and resubmit.
        </p>
        <Textarea
          label="Feedback for instructor"
          placeholder="Explain what needs to change before this course can be approved..."
          rows={5}
          value={reason}
          onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)}
        />
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\dashboard\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  Users,
  DollarSign,
  ClipboardCheck,
  LifeBuoy,
  ArrowRight
} from "lucide-react";
import { LineChart, Line, XAxis, YAxis, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer, CartesianGrid } from "recharts";
import Link from "next/link";
import { KpiCard, StatusPill, ShimmerKpiCard, ShimmerChart, Shimmer } from "@saachi/ui";

const REVENUE_TREND = [
  { day: "Mon", revenue: 1240 },
  { day: "Tue", revenue: 1890 },
  { day: "Wed", revenue: 1510 },
  { day: "Thu", revenue: 2230 },
  { day: "Fri", revenue: 2670 },
  { day: "Sat", revenue: 1980 },
  { day: "Sun", revenue: 2410 }
];

const ALERT_FEED = [
  { id: "al_1", label: "3 courses pending moderation review", tone: "warning" as const, href: "/courses/moderation" },
  { id: "al_2", label: "1 refund request awaiting approval", tone: "error" as const, href: "/finance/refunds" },
  { id: "al_3", label: "2 support tickets breaching SLA", tone: "error" as const, href: "/support/tickets" },
  { id: "al_4", label: "Weekly payout batch ready to process", tone: "info" as const, href: "/finance/payouts" }
];

export default function AdminDashboardPage() {
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setIsLoading(false), 700);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, []);

  const kpis = [
    { label: "New signups today", value: "142", icon: Users, delta: { value: "+12%", direction: "up" as const } },
    { label: "Revenue today", value: "$4,380", icon: DollarSign, delta: { value: "+8%", direction: "up" as const } },
    { label: "Pending approvals", value: "3", icon: ClipboardCheck, delta: { value: "-2", direction: "down" as const } },
    { label: "Open tickets", value: "7", icon: LifeBuoy, delta: { value: "+1", direction: "up" as const } }
  ];

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1200px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-[24px] font-semibold text-ink">Dashboard</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Platform-wide overview and pending operational items.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {isLoading
          ? Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => <ShimmerKpiCard key={i} />)
          : kpis.map((kpi) => <KpiCard key={kpi.label} {...kpi} />)}
      </div>

      <div className="mb-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1.4fr_1fr]">
        {isLoading ? (
          <ShimmerChart />
        ) : (
          <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
            <div className="mb-1 text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Revenue - last 7 days</div>
            <p className="mb-5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">Gross transaction volume across all payment gateways.</p>
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>
              <LineChart data={REVENUE_TREND}>
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="var(--color-line)" />
                <XAxis dataKey="day" stroke="var(--color-ink-soft)" fontSize={12} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} />
                <YAxis stroke="var(--color-ink-soft)" fontSize={12} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} />
                <Tooltip
                  contentStyle={{
                    background: "var(--color-card)",
                    border: "1px solid var(--color-line)",
                    borderRadius: 10,
                    fontSize: 12,
                    color: "var(--color-ink)"
                  }}
                />
                <Line type="monotone" dataKey="revenue" stroke="var(--color-ink)" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
              </LineChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
          <div className="mb-4 text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Needs attention</div>
          {isLoading ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
                <Shimmer key={i} className="h-11 w-full" />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              {ALERT_FEED.map((alert) => (
                <Link
                  key={alert.id}
                  href={alert.href}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-control border border-line px-3.5 py-3 text-[13px] text-ink transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <StatusPill label={alert.tone} tone={alert.tone} />
                    <span className="text-[12.5px]">{alert.label}</span>
                  </div>
                  <ArrowRight size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} className="shrink-0 text-ink-soft" />
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\finance\refunds\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Undo2, CheckCircle2, XCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { useAdminRefunds, useResolveRefund, type AdminRefund } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Select, StatusPill, ShimmerTableRow, EmptyState, Modal } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Pending", value: "pending" },
  { label: "Approved", value: "approved" },
  { label: "Rejected", value: "rejected" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<AdminRefund["status"], "success" | "warning" | "error"> = {
  pending: "warning",
  approved: "success",
  rejected: "error"
};

export default function RefundManagementPage() {
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("");
  const { data: refunds, isLoading } = useAdminRefunds({ status: statusFilter || undefined });
  const resolveRefund = useResolveRefund();
  const [viewTarget, setViewTarget] = useState<AdminRefund | null>(null);

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <Undo2 size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Refund Management</h1>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Learner refund requests - approving reverses the transaction and clawbacks instructor earnings.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="w-[180px]">
          <Select placeholder="All statuses" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={statusFilter} onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={4} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : !refunds || refunds.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<Undo2 size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />} title="No refund requests found" description="Try a different status filter." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {refunds.map((refund) => (
            <Card key={refund.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <p className="truncate text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{refund.courseTitle}</p>
                  <StatusPill label={refund.status} tone={STATUS_TONE[refund.status]} />
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                  {refund.learnerName} - Requested {formatDate(refund.requestedAt)} - {refund.transactionId}
                </p>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setViewTarget(refund)}
                  className="mt-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-ink underline decoration-line-strong underline-offset-2 hover:decoration-ink"
                >
                  View reason
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-4">
                <span className="font-mono text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">
                  {formatCurrency(refund.amount, refund.currency)}
                </span>
                {refund.status === "pending" && (
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => resolveRefund.mutate({ id: refund.id, status: "rejected" })}>
                      <XCircle size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Reject
                    </Button>
                    <Button size="sm" onClick={() => resolveRefund.mutate({ id: refund.id, status: "approved" })}>
                      <CheckCircle2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Approve
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <Modal open={Boolean(viewTarget)} onClose={() => setViewTarget(null)} title="Refund reason">
        <p className="mb-1 text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">{viewTarget?.courseTitle}</p>
        <p className="text-[14px] text-ink-soft">{viewTarget?.reason}</p>
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\finance\tax-settings\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Percent, Plus, Pencil, Check, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useAdminTaxRules, useUpdateTaxRule, useCreateTaxRule, type TaxRule } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Input, Modal, ShimmerTableRow } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function TaxSettingsPage() {
  const { data: rules, isLoading } = useAdminTaxRules();
  const updateRule = useUpdateTaxRule();
  const createRule = useCreateTaxRule();

  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<TaxRule | null>(null);
  const [editRate, setEditRate] = useState(0);
  const [editActive, setEditActive] = useState(true);

  const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false);
  const [newRegion, setNewRegion] = useState("");
  const [newRate, setNewRate] = useState(0);
  const [newAppliesTo, setNewAppliesTo] = useState("");

  const openEdit = (rule: TaxRule) => {
    setEditing(rule);
    setEditRate(rule.ratePercent);
    setEditActive(rule.active);
  };

  const handleSaveEdit = () => {
    if (!editing) return;
    updateRule.mutate({ id: editing.id, ratePercent: editRate, active: editActive });
    setEditing(null);
  };

  const handleCreate = () => {
    if (!newRegion.trim()) return;
    createRule.mutate({ region: newRegion.trim(), ratePercent: newRate, appliesTo: newAppliesTo.trim() || "All course sales" });
    setShowCreate(false);
    setNewRegion("");
    setNewRate(0);
    setNewAppliesTo("");
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[900px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <Percent size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Tax / VAT Settings</h1>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Region-based tax rules applied at checkout.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setShowCreate(true)}>
          <Plus size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> New rule
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
        {isLoading ? (
          Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={3} />)
        ) : (
          rules?.map((rule) => (
            <div key={rule.id} className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-line px-5 py-4 last:border-0 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{rule.region}</p>
                  <span
                    className={
                      rule.active
                        ? "rounded-full bg-success-tint px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-success"
                        : "rounded-full bg-paper-dim px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-ink-soft"
                    }
                  >
                    {rule.active ? "Active" : "Inactive"}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{rule.appliesTo}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-4">
                <span className="font-mono text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">{rule.ratePercent}%</span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => openEdit(rule)}
                  className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                >
                  <Pencil size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>

      <Modal
        open={Boolean(editing)}
        onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
        title={`Edit tax rule - ${editing?.region ?? ""}`}
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={handleSaveEdit} disabled={updateRule.isPending}>
              Save changes
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input
            label="Rate (%)"
            type="number"
            min={0}
            max={100}
            value={editRate}
            onChange={(e) => setEditRate(Number(e.target.value))}
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setEditActive((v) => !v)}
            className="flex w-fit items-center gap-2 rounded-control border border-line-strong px-3.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink hover:border-ink"
          >
            {editActive ? <Check size={14} strokeWidth={2} className="text-success" /> : <X size={14} strokeWidth={2} className="text-ink-soft" />}
            {editActive ? "Active" : "Inactive"} - click to toggle
          </button>
        </div>
      </Modal>

      <Modal
        open={showCreate}
        onClose={() => setShowCreate(false)}
        title="New tax rule"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setShowCreate(false)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={handleCreate} disabled={!newRegion.trim() || createRule.isPending}>
              Create rule
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Region" placeholder="e.g. Canada" value={newRegion} onChange={(e) => setNewRegion(e.target.value)} />
          <Input label="Rate (%)" type="number" min={0} max={100} value={newRate} onChange={(e) => setNewRate(Number(e.target.value))} />
          <Input label="Applies to" placeholder="e.g. Digital goods (GST)" value={newAppliesTo} onChange={(e) => setNewAppliesTo(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\finance\transactions\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Search, Receipt, Download } from "lucide-react";
import type { ColumnDef } from "@tanstack/react-table";
import { useAdminTransactions, type AdminTransaction } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { DataTable, StatusPill, Select, Button } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Completed", value: "completed" },
  { label: "Pending", value: "pending" },
  { label: "Failed", value: "failed" },
  { label: "Refunded", value: "refunded" }
];

const GATEWAY_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Stripe", value: "stripe" },
  { label: "PayPal", value: "paypal" },
  { label: "Easypaisa", value: "easypaisa" },
  { label: "JazzCash", value: "jazzcash" },
  { label: "Bank Transfer", value: "bank_transfer" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<AdminTransaction["status"], "success" | "warning" | "error" | "info"> = {
  completed: "success",
  pending: "warning",
  failed: "error",
  refunded: "info"
};

export default function TransactionsLedgerPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
  const [gateway, setGateway] = useState("");

  const { data, isLoading } = useAdminTransactions({
    search: search || undefined,
    status: status || undefined,
    gateway: gateway || undefined
  });

  const columns = useMemo<ColumnDef<AdminTransaction, any>[]>(
    () => [
      {
        header: "Transaction",
        accessorKey: "id",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <div>
            <div className="font-mono text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{row.original.id}</div>
            <div className="max-w-[220px] truncate font-medium text-ink">{row.original.courseTitle}</div>
          </div>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Learner",
        accessorKey: "learnerName",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{getValue()}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Instructor",
        accessorKey: "instructorName",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{getValue()}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Amount",
        accessorKey: "amount",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-ink">
            {formatCurrency(row.original.amount, row.original.currency)}
          </span>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Commission",
        accessorKey: "commission",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <span className="font-mono text-ink-soft">
            {formatCurrency(row.original.commission, row.original.currency)}
          </span>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Gateway",
        accessorKey: "gateway",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="capitalize text-ink-soft">{(getValue() as string).replace("_", " ")}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Status",
        accessorKey: "status",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => {
          const value = getValue() as AdminTransaction["status"];
          return <StatusPill label={value} tone={STATUS_TONE[value]} />;
        }
      },
      {
        header: "Date",
        accessorKey: "createdAt",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatDate(getValue() as string)}</span>
      }
    ],
    []
  );

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1240px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <Receipt size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Transactions Ledger</h1>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Full financial transaction history across all payment gateways.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button size="sm" variant="secondary">
          <Download size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Export CSV
        </Button>
      </div>

      <DataTable
        columns={columns}
        data={data ?? []}
        isLoading={isLoading}
        emptyTitle="No transactions found"
        emptyDescription="Try adjusting your search or filters."
        toolbar={
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft" />
              <input
                placeholder="Search by ID, course, learner, or instructor"
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                className="h-10 w-full rounded-control border border-line bg-card pl-9 pr-3 text-[13px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:border-line-strong"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
              <Select placeholder="All statuses" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
              <Select placeholder="All gateways" options={GATEWAY_OPTIONS} value={gateway} onChange={(e) => setGateway(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
          </div>
        }
      />
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\marketing\banners\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Image as ImageIcon, Plus, Pencil, Trash2, ArrowUp, ArrowDown } from "lucide-react";
import {
  useAdminBanners,
  useCreateBanner,
  useUpdateBanner,
  useDeleteBanner,
  useReorderBanner,
  type AdminBanner
} from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Input, Textarea, Select, Modal, StatusPill, ShimmerKpiCard, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Draft", value: "draft" },
  { label: "Scheduled", value: "scheduled" },
  { label: "Active", value: "active" },
  { label: "Expired", value: "expired" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<AdminBanner["status"], "success" | "warning" | "error" | "info" | "neutral"> = {
  active: "success",
  scheduled: "info",
  expired: "error",
  draft: "neutral"
};

type FormState = {
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  imageUrl: string;
  linkUrl: string;
  status: AdminBanner["status"];
  startsAt: string;
  endsAt: string;
};

const EMPTY_FORM: FormState = {
  title: "",
  subtitle: "",
  imageUrl: "",
  linkUrl: "",
  status: "draft",
  startsAt: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  endsAt: new Date(Date.now() + 14 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 10)
};

export default function HomepageBannersPage() {
  const { data: banners, isLoading } = useAdminBanners();
  const createBanner = useCreateBanner();
  const updateBanner = useUpdateBanner();
  const deleteBanner = useDeleteBanner();
  const reorderBanner = useReorderBanner();

  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<AdminBanner | null>(null);
  const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState<FormState>(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<AdminBanner | null>(null);

  const openCreate = () => {
    setForm(EMPTY_FORM);
    setShowCreate(true);
  };

  const openEdit = (banner: AdminBanner) => {
    setEditing(banner);
    setForm({
      title: banner.title,
      subtitle: banner.subtitle ?? "",
      imageUrl: banner.imageUrl,
      linkUrl: banner.linkUrl,
      status: banner.status,
      startsAt: banner.startsAt.slice(0, 10),
      endsAt: banner.endsAt.slice(0, 10)
    });
  };

  const closeModals = () => {
    setShowCreate(false);
    setEditing(null);
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    if (!form.title.trim()) return;
    const payload = {
      title: form.title.trim(),
      subtitle: form.subtitle.trim() || undefined,
      imageUrl: form.imageUrl.trim() || `https://picsum.photos/seed/${encodeURIComponent(form.title)}/1200/400`,
      linkUrl: form.linkUrl.trim() || "/",
      status: form.status,
      startsAt: new Date(form.startsAt).toISOString(),
      endsAt: new Date(form.endsAt).toISOString()
    };
    if (editing) {
      updateBanner.mutate({ id: editing.id, ...payload });
    } else {
      createBanner.mutate(payload);
    }
    closeModals();
  };

  const handleDelete = () => {
    if (!deleteTarget) return;
    deleteBanner.mutate(deleteTarget.id);
    setDeleteTarget(null);
  };

  const modalOpen = showCreate || Boolean(editing);

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <ImageIcon size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Homepage Banners</h1>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Create, schedule, and reorder homepage promotional banners.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button size="sm" onClick={openCreate}>
          <Plus size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> New banner
        </Button>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerKpiCard key={i} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : !banners || banners.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<ImageIcon size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />} title="No banners yet" description="Create your first homepage banner." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {banners.map((banner, index) => (
            <Card key={banner.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
              <div className="h-[64px] w-[140px] shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-control border border-line bg-paper-dim">
                {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
                <img src={banner.imageUrl} alt={banner.title} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
              </div>

              <div className="flex-1">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{banner.title}</p>
                  <StatusPill label={banner.status} tone={STATUS_TONE[banner.status]} />
                </div>
                {banner.subtitle && <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">{banner.subtitle}</p>}
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-ink-soft/70">
                  {formatDate(banner.startsAt)} - {formatDate(banner.endsAt)} - links to {banner.linkUrl}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={index === 0}
                  onClick={() => reorderBanner.mutate({ id: banner.id, direction: "up" })}
                  className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink disabled:opacity-30"
                >
                  <ArrowUp size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={index === banners.length - 1}
                  onClick={() => reorderBanner.mutate({ id: banner.id, direction: "down" })}
                  className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink disabled:opacity-30"
                >
                  <ArrowDown size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => openEdit(banner)}
                  className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                >
                  <Pencil size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(banner)}
                  className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error"
                >
                  <Trash2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <Modal
        open={modalOpen}
        onClose={closeModals}
        title={editing ? "Edit banner" : "New banner"}
        size="lg"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={closeModals}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={handleSave} disabled={!form.title.trim() || createBanner.isPending || updateBanner.isPending}>
              {editing ? "Save changes" : "Create banner"}
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Title" value={form.title} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, title: e.target.value })} placeholder="e.g. Founding Instructor Spots Open" />
          <Textarea label="Subtitle" value={form.subtitle} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, subtitle: e.target.value })} rows={2} placeholder="Optional supporting line" />
          <Input label="Image URL" value={form.imageUrl} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, imageUrl: e.target.value })} placeholder="Leave blank to use a placeholder image" />
          <Input label="Link URL" value={form.linkUrl} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, linkUrl: e.target.value })} placeholder="/courses" />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
            <Select label="Status" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={form.status} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, status: e.target.value as AdminBanner["status"] })} />
            <Input label="Starts" type="date" value={form.startsAt} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, startsAt: e.target.value })} />
            <Input label="Ends" type="date" value={form.endsAt} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, endsAt: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </Modal>

      <Modal
        open={Boolean(deleteTarget)}
        onClose={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}
        title="Delete banner?"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button variant="danger" onClick={handleDelete} disabled={deleteBanner.isPending}>
              Delete
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <p className="text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
          This will remove <span className="font-semibold text-ink">{deleteTarget?.title}</span> from the homepage rotation.
        </p>
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\marketing\coupons\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Ticket, Plus, Trash2, Search } from "lucide-react";
import {
  useAdminCoupons,
  useCreateCoupon,
  useToggleCouponActive,
  useDeleteCoupon,
  type AdminCoupon
} from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Input, Select, Modal, ShimmerTableRow, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const TYPE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Percent off", value: "percent" },
  { label: "Fixed amount off", value: "fixed" }
];

const SCOPE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Platform-wide", value: "platform_wide" },
  { label: "Category", value: "category" },
  { label: "Course", value: "course" }
];

export default function CouponManagementPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const { data: coupons, isLoading } = useAdminCoupons({ search: search || undefined });
  const createCoupon = useCreateCoupon();
  const toggleActive = useToggleCouponActive();
  const deleteCoupon = useDeleteCoupon();

  const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false);
  const [code, setCode] = useState("");
  const [type, setType] = useState<AdminCoupon["type"]>("percent");
  const [value, setValue] = useState(10);
  const [scope, setScope] = useState<AdminCoupon["scope"]>("platform_wide");
  const [usageLimit, setUsageLimit] = useState(100);
  const [expiresAt, setExpiresAt] = useState(new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 10));
  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<AdminCoupon | null>(null);

  const resetForm = () => {
    setCode("");
    setType("percent");
    setValue(10);
    setScope("platform_wide");
    setUsageLimit(100);
    setExpiresAt(new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 10));
  };

  const handleCreate = () => {
    if (!code.trim()) return;
    createCoupon.mutate({
      code: code.trim().toUpperCase(),
      type,
      value,
      scope,
      usageLimit,
      expiresAt: new Date(expiresAt).toISOString(),
      active: true
    });
    setShowCreate(false);
    resetForm();
  };

  const handleDelete = () => {
    if (!deleteTarget) return;
    deleteCoupon.mutate(deleteTarget.id);
    setDeleteTarget(null);
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1100px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <Ticket size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Coupon Management</h1>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Platform-wide and scoped promotional codes.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setShowCreate(true)}>
          <Plus size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> New coupon
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="relative mb-5 max-w-[320px]">
        <Search size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft" />
        <input
          placeholder="Search coupon code"
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          className="h-10 w-full rounded-control border border-line bg-card pl-9 pr-3 text-[13px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:border-line-strong"
        />
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={5} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : !coupons || coupons.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<Ticket size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />} title="No coupons found" description="Try a different search, or create a new coupon." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {coupons.map((coupon) => {
            const usagePct = coupon.usageLimit > 0 ? Math.round((coupon.usageCount / coupon.usageLimit) * 100) : 0;
            return (
              <Card key={coupon.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <span className="rounded-control border border-line-strong px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">
                      {coupon.code}
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={
                        coupon.active
                          ? "rounded-full bg-success-tint px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-success"
                          : "rounded-full bg-paper-dim px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-ink-soft"
                      }
                    >
                      {coupon.active ? "Active" : "Inactive"}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1.5 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                    {coupon.type === "percent" ? `${coupon.value}% off` : `$${coupon.value} off`} - {coupon.scope.replace("_", " ")} -
                    {" "}{coupon.usageCount}/{coupon.usageLimit} used ({usagePct}%) - expires {formatDate(coupon.expiresAt)}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => toggleActive.mutate(coupon.id)}>
                    {coupon.active ? "Deactivate" : "Activate"}
                  </Button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(coupon)}
                    className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error"
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      <Modal
        open={showCreate}
        onClose={() => setShowCreate(false)}
        title="New coupon"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setShowCreate(false)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={handleCreate} disabled={!code.trim() || createCoupon.isPending}>
              Create coupon
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Coupon code" value={code} onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value.toUpperCase())} placeholder="e.g. SUMMER20" />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <Select label="Discount type" options={TYPE_OPTIONS} value={type} onChange={(e) => setType(e.target.value as AdminCoupon["type"])} />
            <Input
              label={type === "percent" ? "Percent off" : "Amount off ($)"}
              type="number"
              min={0}
              value={value}
              onChange={(e) => setValue(Number(e.target.value))}
            />
          </div>
          <Select label="Scope" options={SCOPE_OPTIONS} value={scope} onChange={(e) => setScope(e.target.value as AdminCoupon["scope"])} />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <Input label="Usage limit" type="number" min={1} value={usageLimit} onChange={(e) => setUsageLimit(Number(e.target.value))} />
            <Input label="Expires" type="date" value={expiresAt} onChange={(e) => setExpiresAt(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </Modal>

      <Modal
        open={Boolean(deleteTarget)}
        onClose={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}
        title="Delete coupon?"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button variant="danger" onClick={handleDelete} disabled={deleteCoupon.isPending}>
              Delete
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <p className="text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
          This will permanently remove <span className="font-semibold text-ink">{deleteTarget?.code}</span>. It can no
          longer be applied at checkout.
        </p>
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Megaphone, Crown } from "lucide-react";
import { useAdminPromotionSales, type PromotionSale } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Card, StatusPill, ShimmerKpiCard, ShimmerTableRow, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const TIER_TONE: Record<PromotionSale["tier"], "warning" | "info" | "success"> = {
  bronze: "warning",
  silver: "info",
  gold: "success"
};

export default function PromotionSalesPage() {
  const { data: sales, isLoading } = useAdminPromotionSales();

  const now = Date.now();
  const active = (sales ?? []).filter((s) => new Date(s.expiresAt).getTime() > now);
  const totalRevenue = (sales ?? []).reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.amount, 0);
  const tierCounts = {
    gold: (sales ?? []).filter((s) => s.tier === "gold").length,
    silver: (sales ?? []).filter((s) => s.tier === "silver").length,
    bronze: (sales ?? []).filter((s) => s.tier === "bronze").length
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1100px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
          <Megaphone size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Promotion Package Sales</h1>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Instructor purchases of Bronze/Silver/Gold visibility boosts.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerKpiCard key={i} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <Card className="p-5">
            <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">Total revenue</p>
            <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{formatCurrency(totalRevenue, "USD")}</p>
          </Card>
          <Card className="p-5">
            <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">Currently active</p>
            <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{active.length}</p>
          </Card>
          <Card className="p-5">
            <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
              <Crown size={12} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Gold sold
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{tierCounts.gold}</p>
          </Card>
          <Card className="p-5">
            <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">Silver + Bronze sold</p>
            <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{tierCounts.silver + tierCounts.bronze}</p>
          </Card>
        </div>
      )}

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={4} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : !sales || sales.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<Megaphone size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />} title="No promotion sales yet" description="Boost package purchases will show up here." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {sales.map((sale) => {
            const isActive = new Date(sale.expiresAt).getTime() > now;
            return (
              <Card key={sale.id} className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <p className="truncate text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{sale.courseTitle}</p>
                    <StatusPill label={sale.tier} tone={TIER_TONE[sale.tier]} />
                    {!isActive && <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-ink-soft">Expired</span>}
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                    {sale.instructorName} - purchased {formatDate(sale.purchasedAt)} - {isActive ? "expires" : "expired"} {formatDate(sale.expiresAt)}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <span className="shrink-0 font-mono text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
                  {formatCurrency(sale.amount, sale.currency)}
                </span>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { MessagesSquare, Trash2, ShieldAlert, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useFlaggedForumPosts, useResolveFlaggedForumPost, type FlaggedForumPost } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Select, StatusPill, ShimmerTableRow, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatRelativeTime } from "@saachi/utils";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Pending", value: "pending" },
  { label: "Removed", value: "removed" },
  { label: "Dismissed", value: "dismissed" },
  { label: "Warned", value: "warned" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<FlaggedForumPost["status"], "warning" | "error" | "neutral" | "info"> = {
  pending: "warning",
  removed: "error",
  dismissed: "neutral",
  warned: "info"
};

export default function FlaggedForumPostsPage() {

  const { data: posts, isLoading } = useFlaggedForumPosts({ status: statusFilter || undefined });
  const resolve = useResolveFlaggedForumPost();

  return (

            <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Flagged Forum Posts</h1>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Community Q&A posts reported for review.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="w-[180px]">
          <Select placeholder="All statuses" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={statusFilter} onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={4} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : !posts || posts.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<MessagesSquare size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />} title="Nothing flagged" description="No forum posts match this filter." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {posts.map((post) => (
            <Card key={post.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{post.authorName}</p>
                  <StatusPill label={post.status} tone={STATUS_TONE[post.status]} />
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                  in {post.courseTitle} - {formatRelativeTime(post.createdAt)}
                </p>
              </div>

              <p className="rounded-control border border-line bg-paper-dim p-3.5 text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink">
                {post.body}
              </p>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                <ShieldAlert size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} className="shrink-0 text-error" />
                Flagged by {post.flaggedBy} - reason: {post.flagReason}
              </div>

              {post.status === "pending" && (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => resolve.mutate({ id: post.id, status: "dismissed" })}>
                    <CheckCircle2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Dismiss flag
                  </Button>
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => resolve.mutate({ id: post.id, status: "warned" })}>
                    Warn user
                  </Button>
                  <Button size="sm" variant="danger" onClick={() => resolve.mutate({ id: post.id, status: "removed" })}>
                    <Trash2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Remove post
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { MessageSquareWarning, Trash2, ShieldAlert, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useFlaggedReviews, useResolveFlaggedReview, type FlaggedReview } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Select, StatusPill, ShimmerTableRow, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatRelativeTime } from "@saachi/utils";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Pending", value: "pending" },
  { label: "Removed", value: "removed" },
  { label: "Dismissed", value: "dismissed" },
  { label: "Warned", value: "warned" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<FlaggedReview["status"], "warning" | "error" | "neutral" | "info"> = {
  pending: "warning",
  removed: "error",
  dismissed: "neutral",
  warned: "info"
};

export default function FlaggedReviewsPage() {
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("pending");
  const { data: reviews, isLoading } = useFlaggedReviews({ status: statusFilter || undefined });
  const resolve = useResolveFlaggedReview();

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <MessageSquareWarning size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Flagged Reviews</h1>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Course reviews reported by instructors, learners, or automated filters.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="w-[180px]">
          <Select placeholder="All statuses" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={statusFilter} onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={4} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : !reviews || reviews.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<MessageSquareWarning size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />} title="Nothing flagged" description="No reviews match this filter." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {reviews.map((review) => (
            <Card key={review.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{review.authorName}</p>
                    <StatusPill label={review.status} tone={STATUS_TONE[review.status]} />
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                    on {review.courseTitle} - {"\u2605".repeat(review.rating)}{"\u2606".repeat(5 - review.rating)} - {formatRelativeTime(review.createdAt)}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <p className="rounded-control border border-line bg-paper-dim p-3.5 text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink">
                {review.comment}
              </p>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                <ShieldAlert size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} className="shrink-0 text-error" />
                Flagged by {review.flaggedBy} - reason: {review.flagReason}
              </div>

              {review.status === "pending" && (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => resolve.mutate({ id: review.id, status: "dismissed" })}>
                    <CheckCircle2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Dismiss flag
                  </Button>
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => resolve.mutate({ id: review.id, status: "warned" })}>
                    Warn user
                  </Button>
                  <Button size="sm" variant="danger" onClick={() => resolve.mutate({ id: review.id, status: "removed" })}>
                    <Trash2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Remove review
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Search, LifeBuoy, AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import type { ColumnDef } from "@tanstack/react-table";
import { useAdminTickets, type SupportTicket } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { DataTable, StatusPill, Select } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatRelativeTime } from "@saachi/utils";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Open", value: "open" },
  { label: "In progress", value: "in_progress" },
  { label: "Resolved", value: "resolved" },
  { label: "Closed", value: "closed" }
];

const PRIORITY_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Low", value: "low" },
  { label: "Medium", value: "medium" },
  { label: "High", value: "high" },
  { label: "Urgent", value: "urgent" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<SupportTicket["status"], "warning" | "info" | "success" | "neutral"> = {
  open: "warning",
  in_progress: "info",
  resolved: "success",
  closed: "neutral"
};

const PRIORITY_TONE: Record<SupportTicket["priority"], "neutral" | "info" | "warning" | "error"> = {
  low: "neutral",
  medium: "info",
  high: "warning",
  urgent: "error"
};

export default function SupportTicketQueuePage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
  const [priority, setPriority] = useState("");

  const { data, isLoading } = useAdminTickets({
    search: search || undefined,
    status: status || undefined,
    priority: priority || undefined
  });

  const columns = useMemo<ColumnDef<SupportTicket, any>[]>(
    () => [
      {
        header: "Subject",
        accessorKey: "subject",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <div className="max-w-[280px]">
            <div className="truncate font-medium text-ink">{row.original.subject}</div>
            <div className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{row.original.requesterName} - {row.original.category}</div>
          </div>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Priority",
        accessorKey: "priority",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => {
          const value = getValue() as SupportTicket["priority"];
          return <StatusPill label={value} tone={PRIORITY_TONE[value]} />;
        }
      },
      {
        header: "Status",
        accessorKey: "status",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => {
          const value = getValue() as SupportTicket["status"];
          return <StatusPill label={value.replace("_", " ")} tone={STATUS_TONE[value]} />;
        }
      },
      {
        header: "Assigned to",
        accessorKey: "assignedTo",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{(getValue() as string) ?? "Unassigned"}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "SLA",
        accessorKey: "slaDueAt",
        cell: ({ row }) => {
          const overdue = new Date(row.original.slaDueAt).getTime() < Date.now() && row.original.status !== "resolved" && row.original.status !== "closed";
          return overdue ? (
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-medium text-error">
              <AlertTriangle size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Overdue
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatRelativeTime(row.original.slaDueAt)}</span>
          );
        }
      },
      {
        header: "Created",
        accessorKey: "createdAt",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatRelativeTime(getValue() as string)}</span>
      }
    ],
    []
  );

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1200px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
          <LifeBuoy size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Support Ticket Queue</h1>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Learner and instructor support requests, tracked against SLA.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <DataTable
        columns={columns}
        data={data ?? []}
        isLoading={isLoading}
        emptyTitle="No tickets found"
        emptyDescription="Try adjusting your search or filters."
        onRowClick={(row) => router.push(`/support/tickets/${row.id}`)}
        toolbar={
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft" />
              <input
                placeholder="Search by subject or requester"
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                className="h-10 w-full rounded-control border border-line bg-card pl-9 pr-3 text-[13px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:border-line-strong"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
              <Select placeholder="All statuses" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
              <Select placeholder="All priorities" options={PRIORITY_OPTIONS} value={priority} onChange={(e) => setPriority(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
          </div>
        }
      />
    </main>
  );
}
