"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Search, UserCog } from "lucide-react";
import type { ColumnDef } from "@tanstack/react-table";
import { useAdminUserList, type AdminUser } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { DataTable, StatusPill, Select } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const ROLE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Learner", value: "learner" },
  { label: "Instructor", value: "instructor" },
  { label: "Corporate", value: "corporate" },
  { label: "Admin", value: "admin" }
];

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Active", value: "active" },
  { label: "Suspended", value: "suspended" },
  { label: "Banned", value: "banned" },
  { label: "Pending", value: "pending" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<AdminUser["status"], "success" | "warning" | "error" | "info"> = {
  active: "success",
  suspended: "warning",
  banned: "error",
  pending: "info"
};

export default function AdminUsersPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [role, setRole] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");

  const { data, isLoading } = useAdminUserList({
    search: search || undefined,
    role: role || undefined,
    status: status || undefined
  });

  const columns = useMemo<ColumnDef<AdminUser, any>[]>(
    () => [
      {
        header: "Name",
        accessorKey: "fullName",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full border border-line bg-paper-dim text-[12px] font-semibold text-ink-soft">
              {row.original.avatarUrl ? (
                // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                <img src={row.original.avatarUrl} alt={row.original.fullName} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
              ) : (
                row.original.fullName.charAt(0)
              )}
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="font-medium text-ink">{row.original.fullName}</div>
              <div className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{row.original.email}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Role",
        accessorKey: "role",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="capitalize text-ink-soft">{getValue()}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Status",
        accessorKey: "status",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => {
          const value = getValue() as AdminUser["status"];
          return <StatusPill label={value} tone={STATUS_TONE[value]} />;
        }
      },
      {
        header: "Location",
        accessorKey: "location",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{getValue() ?? "-"}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Joined",
        accessorKey: "joinedAt",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatDate(getValue() as string)}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Last active",
        accessorKey: "lastActiveAt",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatDate(getValue() as string)}</span>
      }
    ],
    []
  );

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1200px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
          <UserCog size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">User Management</h1>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Learners, instructors, and corporate accounts.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <DataTable
        columns={columns}
        data={data ?? []}
        isLoading={isLoading}
        emptyTitle="No users found"
        emptyDescription="Try adjusting your search or filters."
        onRowClick={(row) => router.push(`/users/${row.id}`)}
        toolbar={
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft" />
              <input
                placeholder="Search by name or email"
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                className="h-10 w-full rounded-control border border-line bg-card pl-9 pr-3 text-[13px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:border-line-strong"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
              <Select placeholder="All roles" options={ROLE_OPTIONS} value={role} onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
              <Select placeholder="All statuses" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
          </div>
        }
      />
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Input, ThemeToggle } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function AdminLoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    document.cookie = "saachi_admin_session=demo_token; path=/; max-age=" + 60 * 60 * 24 * 7;
    setTimeout(() => router.push("/dashboard"), 500);
  };

  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-paper px-4">
      <div className="absolute right-5 top-5">
        <ThemeToggle />
      </div>

      <Card className="w-full max-w-[400px] p-8">
        <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col items-center text-center">
          <span className="mb-4 flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-ink text-[15px] font-bold text-ink">
            S
          </span>
          <h1 className="text-[20px] font-semibold text-ink">Saachi AI - Admin</h1>
          <p className="mt-1.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Internal staff access only. 2FA required.</p>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Email" type="email" placeholder="you@saachi.ai" required />
          <Input label="Password" type="password" placeholder="********" required />
          <Button type="submit" size="lg" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? "Verifying..." : "Continue"}
          </Button>
        </form>

        <div className="mt-5 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
          <ShieldCheck size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          Protected by mandatory two-factor authentication
        </div>
      </Card>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\_components\AdminShell.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { Menu, X, LogOut, Search, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import { ThemeToggle } from "@saachi/ui";
import { ADMIN_NAV } from "../lib/nav-config";

function cx(...parts: Array<string | false | undefined>) {
  return parts.filter(Boolean).join(" ");
}

export function AdminShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(false);

  const sidebarContent = (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <div className={cx("flex items-center gap-2.5 px-5 py-6", collapsed && "justify-center px-0")}>
        <span className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-ink text-[13px] font-bold text-ink">
          S
        </span>
        {!collapsed && (
          <div>
            <div className="text-[14px] font-semibold leading-none text-ink">Saachi AI</div>
            <div className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-ink-soft">Admin</div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <nav className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-5 overflow-y-auto px-3 pb-4">
        {ADMIN_NAV.map((section) => (
          <div key={section.title}>
            {!collapsed && (
              <div className="mb-1.5 px-2.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-ink-soft/60">
                {section.title}
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
              {section.items.map((item) => {
                const active = pathname === item.href || Boolean(pathname?.startsWith(`${item.href}/`));
                return (
                  <Link
                    key={item.href}
                    href={item.href}
                    onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
                    title={collapsed ? item.label : undefined}
                    className={cx(
                      "flex items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-2.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors duration-150 ease-out",
                      collapsed && "justify-center",
                      active ? "bg-accent text-paper" : "text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                    )}
                  >
                    <item.icon size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} className="shrink-0" />
                    {!collapsed && <span className="truncate">{item.label}</span>}
                  </Link>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </nav>

      <div className="border-t border-line px-3 py-3">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setCollapsed((v) => !v)}
          className={cx(
            "mb-1 flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-2.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink",
            collapsed && "justify-center"
          )}
        >
          {collapsed ? <ChevronRight size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} /> : <ChevronLeft size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />}
          {!collapsed && "Collapse"}
        </button>
        <Link
          href="/login"
          className={cx(
            "flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-2.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error",
            collapsed && "justify-center"
          )}
        >
          <LogOut size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          {!collapsed && "Log out"}
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-paper">
      <div
        className={cx(
          "hidden shrink-0 border-r border-line bg-card transition-[width] duration-200 ease-out md:fixed md:inset-y-0 md:flex",
          collapsed ? "w-[76px]" : "w-[248px]"
        )}
      >
        {sidebarContent}
      </div>

      <div
        className={cx(
          "sticky top-0 z-40 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line bg-card/95 px-4 py-3 backdrop-blur-sm transition-[padding] duration-200 ease-out md:px-6",
          collapsed ? "md:pl-[76px]" : "md:pl-[248px]"
        )}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMobileOpen(true)}
            aria-label="Open menu"
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink md:hidden"
          >
            <Menu size={18} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </button>
          <div className="relative hidden sm:block">
            <Search size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft" />
            <input
              placeholder="Search users, courses, tickets..."
              className="h-9 w-[280px] rounded-control border border-line bg-paper pl-9 pr-3 text-[13px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:border-line-strong"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <ThemeToggle />
          <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-line text-[12px] font-semibold text-ink">
            A
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {mobileOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex md:hidden">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-ink/40" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)} />
          <div className="relative flex h-full w-[280px] flex-col bg-card">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
              aria-label="Close menu"
              className="absolute right-3 top-3 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink"
            >
              <X size={18} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            </button>
            {sidebarContent}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className={cx("transition-[padding] duration-200 ease-out", collapsed ? "md:pl-[76px]" : "md:pl-[248px]")}>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\providers.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";

export function Providers({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [queryClient] = useState(
    () =>
      new QueryClient({
        defaultOptions: {
          queries: {
            staleTime: 30 * 1000,
            refetchOnWindowFocus: true,
            retry: 1
          }
        }
      })
  );

  return <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>;
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps\admin\middleware.ts
================================================================================
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";

const PUBLIC_PATHS = ["/login"];

export function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;
  const adminSession = request.cookies.get("saachi_admin_session");

  const isPublic = PUBLIC_PATHS.some((p) => pathname.startsWith(p));

  if (!isPublic && !adminSession) {
    const loginUrl = new URL("/login", request.url);
    return NextResponse.redirect(loginUrl);
  }

  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ["/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico).*)"]
};

/** @type {import("next").NextConfig} */
const nextConfig = {
  reactStrictMode: true,
  transpilePackages: ["@saachi/ui", "@saachi/api-client", "@saachi/utils", "@saachi/store"],
  async rewrites() {
    return [
      {
        source: "/api/proxy/:path*",
        destination: `${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL}/admin/:path*`
      }
    ];
  }
};

module.exports = nextConfig;

FILE: apps\web\app\(auth)\forgot-password\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { forgotPasswordSchema, type ForgotPasswordInput } from "@saachi/utils";
import { apiRequest } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Input } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function ForgotPasswordPage() {
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);
  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors, isSubmitting }
  } = useForm<ForgotPasswordInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(forgotPasswordSchema)
  });

  const onSubmit = async (data: ForgotPasswordInput) => {
    setServerError(null);
    try {
      await apiRequest("/auth/forgot-password", { method: "POST", body: data });
      setSent(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setServerError(err?.message ?? "Something went wrong. Please try again.");
    }
  };

  if (sent) {
    return (
      <Card className="w-full max-w-[420px] p-8">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Check your email</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
          If an account exists for that email, we've sent a password reset link.
        </p>
        <div className="mt-6">
          <Link href="/login" className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink hover:text-gold-dark">
            Back to login
          </Link>
        </div>
      </Card>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Card className="w-full max-w-[420px] p-8">
      <h1 className="font-display text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Reset your password</h1>
      <p className="mb-6 mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Enter your email and we'll send you a reset link.
      </p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Input
          label="Email"
          type="email"
          placeholder="you@example.com"
          {...register("email")}
          error={errors.email?.message}
        />

        {serverError && <p className="text-[13px] text-error">{serverError}</p>}

        <Button type="submit" size="lg" disabled={isSubmitting}>
          {isSubmitting ? "Sendingâ€¦" : "Send Reset Link"}
        </Button>
      </form>

      <div className="mt-5 text-center text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        <Link href="/login" className="hover:text-ink">
          Back to login
        </Link>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(auth)\login\2fa\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { twoFactorSchema, type TwoFactorInput } from "@saachi/utils";
import { Button, Card, Input } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function TwoFactorPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors, isSubmitting }
  } = useForm<TwoFactorInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(twoFactorSchema)
  });

  const onSubmit = async (data: TwoFactorInput) => {
    setServerError(null);
    // NOTE: demo-only. In this build, useLogin never returns requires2fa: true,
    // so this screen is reachable directly for UI/QA purposes. Wire to real
    // Auth API 2FA verification once the backend is available.
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 500));
    if (data.code !== "000000") {
      setServerError("Demo mode: enter 000000 to continue.");
      return;
    }
    router.push("/dashboard");
  };

  return (
    <Card className="w-full max-w-[400px] p-8 text-center">
      <div className="mx-auto mb-4 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-teal-light text-teal">
        <ShieldCheck size={22} />
      </div>
      <h1 className="font-display text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Two-factor verification</h1>
      <p className="mb-6 mt-2 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Enter the 6-digit code from your authenticator app.
      </p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="flex flex-col gap-4 text-left">
        <Input
          label="Verification code"
          placeholder="000000"
          maxLength={6}
          {...register("code")}
          error={errors.code?.message}
        />

        {serverError && <p className="text-[13px] text-error">{serverError}</p>}

        <Button type="submit" size="lg" disabled={isSubmitting}>
          {isSubmitting ? "Verifying..." : "Verify"}
        </Button>
      </form>
    </Card>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(auth)\login\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { loginSchema, type LoginInput } from "@saachi/utils";
import { useLogin } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Input, SocialLoginButtons } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const loginMutation = useLogin();
  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors, isSubmitting }
  } = useForm<LoginInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(loginSchema)
  });

  const onSubmit = async (data: LoginInput) => {
    setServerError(null);
    try {
      const result = await loginMutation.mutateAsync(data);
      if ("requires2fa" in result && result.requires2fa) {
        router.push("/login/2fa");
        return;
      }
      router.push("/dashboard");
    } catch (err: any) {
      setServerError(err?.message ?? "Login failed. Please try again.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <Card className="w-full max-w-[420px] p-8">
      <h1 className="font-display text-[24px] font-semibold text-ink">Welcome back</h1>
      <p className="mb-6 mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Log in to continue learning or teaching on Saachi AI.
      </p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Input
          label="Email"
          type="email"
          placeholder="you@example.com"
          {...register("email")}
          error={errors.email?.message}
        />
        <Input
          label="Password"
          type="password"
          placeholder="********"
          {...register("password")}
          error={errors.password?.message}
        />

        {serverError && <p className="text-[13px] text-error">{serverError}</p>}

        <Button type="submit" size="lg" disabled={isSubmitting || loginMutation.isPending}>
          {isSubmitting || loginMutation.isPending ? "Logging inÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦" : "Log In"}
        </Button>
      </form>

      <div className="mt-6">
        <SocialLoginButtons
          onGoogle={() => onSubmit({ email: "demo.google@saachi.ai", password: "demo-oauth" })}
          onMicrosoft={() => onSubmit({ email: "demo.microsoft@saachi.ai", password: "demo-oauth" })}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 text-center text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        <Link href="/forgot-password" className="hover:text-ink">
          Forgot password?
        </Link>
        <span className="mx-2">Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â·</span>
        <Link href="/register" className="hover:text-ink">
          Create an account
        </Link>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { registerSchema, type RegisterInput } from "@saachi/utils";
import { useRegister } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Input, SocialLoginButtons } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function RegisterPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const registerMutation = useRegister();
  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors, isSubmitting }
  } = useForm<RegisterInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(registerSchema),
    defaultValues: { role: "learner" }
  });

  const onSubmit = async (data: RegisterInput) => {
    setServerError(null);
    try {
      await registerMutation.mutateAsync(data);
      router.push("/dashboard");
    } catch (err: any) {
      setServerError(err?.message ?? "Registration failed. Please try again.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <Card className="w-full max-w-[440px] p-8">
      <h1 className="font-display text-[24px] font-semibold text-ink">Create your account</h1>
      <p className="mb-6 mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Join Saachi AI as a learner. You can apply to become an instructor later.
      </p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Input label="Full name" placeholder="Your name" {...register("fullName")} error={errors.fullName?.message} />
        <Input
          label="Email"
          type="email"
          placeholder="you@example.com"
          {...register("email")}
          error={errors.email?.message}
        />
        <Input
          label="Password"
          type="password"
          placeholder="At least 8 characters"
          {...register("password")}
          error={errors.password?.message}
        />
        <Input
          label="Confirm password"
          type="password"
          placeholder="Repeat your password"
          {...register("confirmPassword")}
          error={errors.confirmPassword?.message}
        />

        {serverError && <p className="text-[13px] text-error">{serverError}</p>}

        <Button type="submit" size="lg" disabled={isSubmitting || registerMutation.isPending}>
          {isSubmitting || registerMutation.isPending ? "Creating accountÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦" : "Create Account"}
        </Button>
      </form>

      <div className="mt-6">
        <SocialLoginButtons
          onGoogle={() =>
            onSubmit({ fullName: "Google User", email: "demo.google@saachi.ai", password: "demo-oauth123", confirmPassword: "demo-oauth123", role: "learner" })
          }
          onMicrosoft={() =>
            onSubmit({ fullName: "Microsoft User", email: "demo.microsoft@saachi.ai", password: "demo-oauth123", confirmPassword: "demo-oauth123", role: "learner" })
          }
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 text-center text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Already have an account?{" "}
        <Link href="/login" className="font-semibold text-ink hover:text-gold-dark">
          Log in
        </Link>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { resetPasswordSchema, type ResetPasswordInput } from "@saachi/utils";
import { Button, Card, Input } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function ResetPasswordPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors, isSubmitting }
  } = useForm<ResetPasswordInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(resetPasswordSchema),
    defaultValues: { token: searchParams.get("token") ?? "demo-token" }
  });

  const onSubmit = async (data: ResetPasswordInput) => {
    setServerError(null);
    // NOTE: demo-only. Replace with apiRequest("/auth/reset-password", ...) once backend exists.
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 500));
    setDone(true);
  };

  if (done) {
    return (
      <Card className="w-full max-w-[420px] p-8 text-center">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Password updated</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
          Your password has been reset. You can now log in with your new password.
        </p>
        <Button size="lg" className="mt-6 w-full" onClick={() => router.push("/login")}>
          Go to login
        </Button>
      </Card>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Card className="w-full max-w-[420px] p-8">
      <h1 className="font-display text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Set a new password</h1>
      <p className="mb-6 mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Choose a strong password for your account.</p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <input type="hidden" {...register("token")} />
        <Input
          label="New password"
          type="password"
          placeholder="At least 8 characters"
          {...register("password")}
          error={errors.password?.message}
        />
        <Input
          label="Confirm new password"
          type="password"
          placeholder="Repeat new password"
          {...register("confirmPassword")}
          error={errors.confirmPassword?.message}
        />

        {serverError && <p className="text-[13px] text-error">{serverError}</p>}

        <Button type="submit" size="lg" disabled={isSubmitting}>
          {isSubmitting ? "Updating..." : "Update password"}
        </Button>
      </form>

      <div className="mt-5 text-center text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        <Link href="/login" className="hover:text-ink">
          Back to login
        </Link>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(auth)\verify-email\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { MailCheck, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function VerifyEmailPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<"pending" | "verified">("pending");

  useEffect(() => {
    // NOTE: demo-only. Replace with apiRequest("/auth/verify-email", ...) using
    // the token from the URL once the backend is available.
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setStatus("verified"), 1200);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, []);

  return (
    <Card className="w-full max-w-[420px] p-8 text-center">
      <div className="mx-auto mb-4 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-teal-light text-teal">
        {status === "pending" ? <Loader2 size={22} className="animate-spin" /> : <MailCheck size={22} />}
      </div>

      {status === "pending" ? (
        <>
          <h1 className="font-display text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Verifying your email...</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">This will only take a moment.</p>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <h1 className="font-display text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Email verified</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
            Your email address has been confirmed. You're all set.
          </p>
          <Button size="lg" className="mt-6 w-full" onClick={() => router.push("/dashboard")}>
            Go to dashboard
          </Button>
        </>
      )}

      <div className="mt-5 text-center text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        <Link href="/login" className="hover:text-ink">
          Back to login
        </Link>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps\web\app\(corporate)\corporate\assignments\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useCorporateStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { DUMMY_COURSES } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Select, Badge, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";
import { Users } from "lucide-react";

export default function CourseAssignmentPage() {
  const { team, assignCourse } = useCorporateStore();
  const [selectedMemberId, setSelectedMemberId] = useState(team[0]?.id ?? "");
  const [selectedCourseId, setSelectedCourseId] = useState(DUMMY_COURSES[0]?.id ?? "");

  const memberOptions = team.map((m) => ({ label: m.name, value: m.id }));
  const courseOptions = DUMMY_COURSES.map((c) => ({ label: c.title, value: c.id }));

  const handleAssign = () => {
    if (!selectedMemberId || !selectedCourseId) return;
    assignCourse(selectedMemberId, selectedCourseId);
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[900px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <h1 className="mb-8 font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Course Assignment</h1>

      {team.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<Users size={22} />} title="No team members yet" description="Invite team members first from Team Roster." />
      ) : (
        <>
          <Card className="mb-8 p-6">
            <h2 className="mb-4 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Assign a course</h2>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end">
              <Select label="Team member" options={memberOptions} value={selectedMemberId} onChange={(e) => setSelectedMemberId(e.target.value)} className="flex-1" />
              <Select label="Course" options={courseOptions} value={selectedCourseId} onChange={(e) => setSelectedCourseId(e.target.value)} className="flex-1" />
              <Button onClick={handleAssign}>Assign</Button>
            </div>
          </Card>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            {team.map((member) => (
              <Card key={member.id} className="p-4">
                <p className="mb-2 text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{member.name}</p>
                {member.assignedCourseIds.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">No courses assigned yet.</p>
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                    {member.assignedCourseIds.map((courseId) => {
                      const course = DUMMY_COURSES.find((c) => c.id === courseId);
                      const completed = member.completedCourseIds.includes(courseId);
                      return (
                        <Badge key={courseId} variant={completed ? "success" : "default"}>
                          {course?.title ?? courseId} {completed ? "- done" : ""}
                        </Badge>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                )}
              </Card>
            ))}
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: apps\web\app\(corporate)\corporate\billing\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { CreditCard, FileText } from "lucide-react";
import { useCorporateStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, Card, Input } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency } from "@saachi/utils";

const SEAT_PRICE = 29;

export default function CorporateBillingPage() {
  const { account, purchaseSeats } = useCorporateStore();
  const [seatCount, setSeatCount] = useState(5);
  const [purchased, setPurchased] = useState(false);

  const handlePurchase = () => {
    purchaseSeats(seatCount);
    setPurchased(true);
    setTimeout(() => setPurchased(false), 2500);
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[800px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <h1 className="mb-8 font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Billing</h1>

      <div className="mb-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <Card className="p-5">
          <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Total seats</p>
          <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{account.seatsPurchased}</p>
        </Card>
        <Card className="p-5">
          <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Seats used</p>
          <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{account.seatsUsed}</p>
        </Card>
      </div>

      <Card className="mb-8 p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Purchase additional seats</h2>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end">
          <Input
            label="Number of seats"
            type="number"
            min={1}
            value={seatCount}
            onChange={(e) => setSeatCount(Number(e.target.value))}
            className="sm:w-[180px]"
          />
          <div className="flex-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
            {seatCount} seats &times; {formatCurrency(SEAT_PRICE, "USD")} ={" "}
            <span className="font-semibold text-ink">{formatCurrency(seatCount * SEAT_PRICE, "USD")}</span>
          </div>
          <Button onClick={handlePurchase}>
            <CreditCard size={16} /> Purchase seats
          </Button>
        </div>
        {purchased && <p className="mt-3 text-[13px] text-teal">Seats purchased (demo) - invoice sent to billing email.</p>}
      </Card>

      <Card className="p-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <FileText size={18} className="text-ink-soft" />
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">PO-based billing</h2>
            <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
              Enterprise clients can request custom invoicing instead of card checkout - contact sales.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>
    </main>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: apps\web\app\(corporate)\corporate\dashboard\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { Users, BookOpen, TrendingUp, CreditCard } from "lucide-react";
import { useCorporateStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Card } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function CorporateDashboardPage() {
  const { account, team } = useCorporateStore();

  const totalAssignments = team.reduce((sum, m) => sum + m.assignedCourseIds.length, 0);
  const totalCompletions = team.reduce((sum, m) => sum + m.completedCourseIds.length, 0);
  const completionRate = totalAssignments > 0 ? Math.round((totalCompletions / totalAssignments) * 100) : 0;
  const activeMembers = team.filter((m) => m.status === "active").length;

  const stats = [
    { label: "Team members", value: `${activeMembers}/${team.length}`, icon: Users },
    { label: "Seats available", value: `${account.seatsPurchased - account.seatsUsed}`, icon: CreditCard },
    { label: "Course assignments", value: String(totalAssignments), icon: BookOpen },
    { label: "Completion rate", value: `${completionRate}%`, icon: TrendingUp }
  ];

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">{account.companyName} Dashboard</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Team learning overview and seat usage.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-10 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {stats.map((stat) => (
          <Card key={stat.label} className="p-5">
            <div className="mb-3 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control bg-paper-dim text-ink">
              <stat.icon size={17} />
            </div>
            <div className="font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{stat.value}</div>
            <div className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{stat.label}</div>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      <h2 className="mb-4 font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">Recent activity</h2>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        {team.slice(0, 5).map((member) => (
          <Card key={member.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{member.name}</p>
              <p className="mt-0.5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
                {member.completedCourseIds.length} of {member.assignedCourseIds.length} assigned courses completed
              </p>
            </div>
            <span className="text-[12px] capitalize text-ink-soft">{member.status}</span>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Download } from "lucide-react";
import { useCorporateStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, Card } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function TeamReportsPage() {
  const { team } = useCorporateStore();

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[900px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Team Reports</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Per-employee completion and compliance overview.</p>
        </div>
        <Button variant="secondary">
          <Download size={15} /> Export CSV
        </Button>
      </div>

      <Card className="overflow-hidden p-0">
        <table className="w-full text-left text-[13px]">
          <thead className="border-b border-line bg-paper-dim text-ink-soft">
            <tr>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold">Name</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold">Status</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold">Assigned</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold">Completed</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold">Completion %</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {team.map((member) => {
              const pct =
                member.assignedCourseIds.length > 0
                  ? Math.round((member.completedCourseIds.length / member.assignedCourseIds.length) * 100)
                  : 0;
              return (
                <tr key={member.id} className="border-b border-line last:border-0">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-ink">{member.name}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 capitalize text-ink-soft">{member.status}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-ink-soft">{member.assignedCourseIds.length}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-ink-soft">{member.completedCourseIds.length}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink">{pct}%</td>
                </tr>
              );
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </Card>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(corporate)\corporate\team\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { UserPlus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useCorporateStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, Card, Input, Badge } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function TeamRosterPage() {
  const { team, account, inviteMember, removeMember } = useCorporateStore();
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");

  const seatsRemaining = account.seatsPurchased - account.seatsUsed;

  const handleInvite = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name.trim() || !email.trim() || seatsRemaining <= 0) return;
    inviteMember(name.trim(), email.trim());
    setName("");
    setEmail("");
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[900px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Team Roster</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
          {seatsRemaining} of {account.seatsPurchased} seats available
        </p>
      </div>

      <Card className="mb-8 p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Invite a team member</h2>
        <form onSubmit={handleInvite} className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end">
          <Input label="Full name" placeholder="Team member's name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} className="flex-1" />
          <Input label="Work email" type="email" placeholder="name@company.com" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} className="flex-1" />
          <Button type="submit" disabled={seatsRemaining <= 0}>
            <UserPlus size={16} /> Invite
          </Button>
        </form>
        {seatsRemaining <= 0 && (
          <p className="mt-3 text-[12px] text-error">No seats remaining - purchase more seats in Billing.</p>
        )}
      </Card>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        {team.map((member) => (
          <Card key={member.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4">
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{member.name}</p>
                <Badge variant={member.status === "active" ? "success" : "default"}>{member.status}</Badge>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{member.email}</p>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => removeMember(member.id)}
              className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error"
              aria-label="Remove member"
            >
              <Trash2 size={15} />
            </button>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: apps\web\app\(corporate)\_components\CorporateShell.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { LayoutDashboard, Users, BookOpen, BarChart3, CreditCard, LogOut, Menu, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession, useLogout } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { useCorporateStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { cn } from "@saachi/ui";

const NAV_ITEMS = [
  { label: "Dashboard", href: "/corporate/dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { label: "Team Roster", href: "/corporate/team", icon: Users },
  { label: "Course Assignment", href: "/corporate/assignments", icon: BookOpen },
  { label: "Reports", href: "/corporate/reports", icon: BarChart3 },
  { label: "Billing", href: "/corporate/billing", icon: CreditCard }
];

export function CorporateShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const logout = useLogout();
  const { account } = useCorporateStore();
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await logout.mutateAsync();
    router.push("/");
  };

  const sidebarContent = (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-6 py-6">
        <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-ink bg-ink font-display text-[15px] font-bold text-white">
          S
        </span>
        <div>
          <div className="font-display text-[15px] font-semibold leading-none text-ink">{account.companyName}</div>
          <div className="mt-1 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-ink-soft">
            Corporate Account
          </div>
        </div>
      </Link>

      <nav className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1 px-3">
        {NAV_ITEMS.map((item) => {
          const active = pathname === item.href || Boolean(pathname && pathname.startsWith(`${item.href}/`));
          return (
            <Link
              key={item.href}
              href={item.href}
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
              className={cn(
                "flex items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-3.5 py-2.5 text-[14px] font-medium transition-colors duration-150 ease-out",
                active ? "bg-paper-dim text-ink" : "text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
              )}
            >
              <item.icon size={18} />
              {item.label}
            </Link>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      <div className="border-t border-line px-3 py-4">
        <div className="mb-3 px-3 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
          {account.seatsUsed} / {account.seatsPurchased} seats used
        </div>
        <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-3 px-3">
          <div className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-teal-light text-[13px] font-semibold text-teal">
            {session?.fullName?.charAt(0) ?? "?"}
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="truncate text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">{session?.fullName ?? "Guest"}</div>
            <div className="truncate text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{session?.email ?? ""}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleLogout}
          disabled={logout.isPending}
          className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-3.5 py-2.5 text-[14px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error"
        >
          <LogOut size={18} />
          {logout.isPending ? "Logging out..." : "Log out"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-paper">
      <div className="hidden w-[260px] shrink-0 border-r border-line bg-card md:fixed md:inset-y-0 md:flex">
        {sidebarContent}
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line bg-card px-4 py-3 md:hidden">
        <span className="font-display text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{account.companyName}</span>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setMobileOpen(true)}
          aria-label="Open menu"
          className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink"
        >
          <Menu size={20} />
        </button>
      </div>

      {mobileOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex md:hidden">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-ink/40" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)} />
          <div className="relative flex h-full w-[280px] flex-col bg-card">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
              aria-label="Close menu"
              className="absolute right-3 top-3 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink"
            >
              <X size={20} />
            </button>
            {sidebarContent}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="md:pl-[260px]">{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { LineChart, Line, XAxis, YAxis, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer, CartesianGrid } from "recharts";

const ENROLLMENT_TREND = [
  { month: "Feb", enrollments: 40 },
  { month: "Mar", enrollments: 62 },
  { month: "Apr", enrollments: 58 },

  { month: "Jun", enrollments: 120 },
  { month: "Jul", enrollments: 148 }
];

================================================================================
FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\instructor\courses\new\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Plus, Trash2, CheckCircle2, PlayCircle, FileText, HelpCircle, Download } from "lucide-react";
import { useCourseBuilderStore, useInstructorCourseStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, Card, Input, Textarea, Select, Checkbox, Badge } from "@saachi/ui";

const STEPS = ["Basics", "Curriculum", "Pricing", "Media", "Review"];

const CATEGORY_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "AI Fundamentals & Prompt Engineering", value: "ai-fundamentals" },
  { label: "AI for Marketing & Sales", value: "ai-marketing" },
  { label: "AI Automation & Workflow Tools", value: "ai-automation" },
  { label: "AI Customer Support & Communication", value: "ai-support" },
  { label: "AI Website & App Development", value: "ai-dev" },
  { label: "AI Integration for Developers", value: "ai-integration" }
];

const LEVEL_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Beginner", value: "beginner" },
  { label: "Intermediate", value: "intermediate" },
  { label: "Advanced", value: "advanced" }
];

const LANGUAGE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "English", value: "en" },
  { label: "Urdu", value: "ur" }
];

const CURRENCY_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "USD", value: "USD" },
  { label: "PKR", value: "PKR" },
  { label: "GBP", value: "GBP" }
];

const LESSON_TYPE_ICON = {
  video: PlayCircle,
  text: FileText,
  quiz: HelpCircle,
  resource: Download
} as const;

export default function CourseWizardPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const wizard = useCourseBuilderStore();
  const instructorCourses = useInstructorCourseStore();
  const { draft, step } = wizard;

  const canGoNext = () => {
    if (step === 1) return draft.title.trim().length >= 5 && draft.categoryId;
    if (step === 2) return draft.modules.length > 0;
    if (step === 3) return draft.price >= 0;
    return true;
  };

  const handlePublish = (status: "draft" | "pending_review") => {
    const totalLessons = draft.modules.reduce((sum, m) => sum + m.lessons.length, 0);
    instructorCourses.addCourse({
      id: `crs_local_${Date.now()}`,
      title: draft.title,
      subtitle: draft.subtitle,
      categoryId: draft.categoryId,
      language: draft.language,
      level: draft.level,
      price: draft.price,
      currency: draft.currency,
      status,
      modules: draft.modules,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      enrollmentCount: 0,
      revenue: 0,
      rating: 0
    });
    wizard.reset();
    router.push("/instructor/courses");
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[800px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <h1 className="mb-2 font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Create a course</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        This demo wizard saves your course locally in the browser (no backend yet).
      </p>

      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center gap-2">
        {STEPS.map((label, i) => {
          const stepNum = i + 1;
          const active = stepNum === step;
          const done = stepNum < step;
          return (
            <div key={label} className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-2">
              <div
                className={[
                  "flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[12px] font-semibold",
                  done ? "bg-teal text-white" : active ? "bg-gold text-ink" : "bg-paper-dim text-ink-soft"
                ].join(" ")}
              >
                {done ? <CheckCircle2 size={15} /> : stepNum}
              </div>
              <span className={active ? "text-[12px] font-semibold text-ink" : "text-[12px] text-ink-soft"}>
                {label}
              </span>
              {stepNum < STEPS.length && <div className="h-px flex-1 bg-line" />}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <Card className="p-6">
        {step === 1 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <Input
              label="Course title"
              placeholder="e.g. Build a WhatsApp AI Auto-Responder"
              value={draft.title}
              onChange={(e) => wizard.updateDraft({ title: e.target.value })}
            />
            <Textarea
              label="Subtitle"
              placeholder="One sentence describing the outcome"
              value={draft.subtitle}
              onChange={(e) => wizard.updateDraft({ subtitle: e.target.value })}
            />
            <Select
              label="Category"
              placeholder="Select a category"
              options={CATEGORY_OPTIONS}
              value={draft.categoryId}
              onChange={(e) => wizard.updateDraft({ categoryId: e.target.value })}
            />
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <Select
                label="Level"
                options={LEVEL_OPTIONS}
                value={draft.level}
                onChange={(e) => wizard.updateDraft({ level: e.target.value as typeof draft.level })}
              />
              <Select
                label="Language"
                options={LANGUAGE_OPTIONS}
                value={draft.language}
                onChange={(e) => wizard.updateDraft({ language: e.target.value })}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 2 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
            {draft.modules.length === 0 && (
              <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Add at least one module to continue.</p>
            )}
            {draft.modules.map((mod) => (
              <div key={mod.id} className="rounded-control border border-line p-4">
                <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Input
                    value={mod.title}
                    onChange={(e) => wizard.updateModuleTitle(mod.id, e.target.value)}
                    className="flex-1"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => wizard.removeModule(mod.id)}
                    className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error"
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={15} />
                  </button>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                  {mod.lessons.map((lesson) => {
                    const Icon = LESSON_TYPE_ICON[lesson.type];
                    return (
                      <div key={lesson.id} className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <Icon size={15} className="shrink-0 text-ink-soft" />
                        <Input
                          value={lesson.title}
                          onChange={(e) => wizard.updateLessonTitle(mod.id, lesson.id, e.target.value)}
                          className="h-9 flex-1"
                        />
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => wizard.removeLesson(mod.id, lesson.id)}
                          className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error"
                        >
                          <Trash2 size={13} />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>

                <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  {(["video", "text", "quiz", "resource"] as const).map((type) => (
                    <button
                      key={type}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => wizard.addLesson(mod.id, type)}
                      className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-line-strong px-3 py-1.5 text-[12px] font-medium text-ink-soft hover:border-ink hover:text-ink"
                    >
                      <Plus size={12} /> {type}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => wizard.addModule()}>
              <Plus size={15} /> Add module
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 3 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <Input
                label="Price"
                type="number"
                min={0}
                value={draft.price}
                onChange={(e) => wizard.updateDraft({ price: Number(e.target.value) })}
              />
              <Select
                label="Currency"
                options={CURRENCY_OPTIONS}
                value={draft.currency}
                onChange={(e) => wizard.updateDraft({ currency: e.target.value as typeof draft.currency })}
              />
            </div>
            <Checkbox
              label="Include this course in Saachi AI Pro subscription"
              checked={draft.subscriptionIncluded}
              onChange={(e) => wizard.updateDraft({ subscriptionIncluded: e.target.checked })}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 4 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <Input
              label="Thumbnail seed (demo)"
              placeholder="e.g. my-course-thumbnail"
              helperText="In demo mode this generates a placeholder image. Real upload wires to Media API later."
              value={draft.thumbnailSeed}
              onChange={(e) => wizard.updateDraft({ thumbnailSeed: e.target.value })}
            />
            {draft.thumbnailSeed && (
              <div className="aspect-video w-full max-w-[360px] overflow-hidden rounded-control bg-paper-dim">
                {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
                <img
                  src={`https://picsum.photos/seed/${encodeURIComponent(draft.thumbnailSeed)}/800/450`}
                  alt="Course thumbnail preview"
                  className="h-full w-full object-cover"
                />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 5 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <h3 className="text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">{draft.title || "Untitled course"}</h3>
            <p className="text-[14px] text-ink-soft">{draft.subtitle}</p>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <Badge>{draft.categoryId || "No category"}</Badge>
              <Badge>{draft.level}</Badge>
              <Badge>{draft.language === "en" ? "English" : "Urdu"}</Badge>
              <Badge variant="gold">
                {draft.currency} {draft.price}
              </Badge>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
              {draft.modules.length} modules, {draft.modules.reduce((s, m) => s + m.lessons.length, 0)} lessons
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>

      <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-between">
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          onClick={() => wizard.setStep(Math.max(1, step - 1))}
          disabled={step === 1}
        >
          Back
        </Button>

        {step < STEPS.length ? (
          <Button onClick={() => wizard.setStep(step + 1)} disabled={!canGoNext()}>
            Continue
          </Button>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex gap-3">
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => handlePublish("draft")}>
              Save as draft
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={() => handlePublish("pending_review")}>Submit for review</Button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { PlusCircle, BookOpen } from "lucide-react";
import { useInstructorCourseStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, Card, Badge, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const STATUS_VARIANT = {
  draft: "default",
  pending_review: "warning",
  published: "success"
} as const;

export default function InstructorCoursesPage() {
  const { courses, removeCourse } = useInstructorCourseStore();

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1100px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">My Courses</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Manage the courses you've created.</p>
        </div>
        <Link href="/instructor/courses/new">
          <Button>
            <PlusCircle size={16} /> Create course
          </Button>
        </Link>
      </div>

      {courses.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<BookOpen size={22} />}
          title="No courses yet"
          description="Start the course creation wizard to publish your first course."
          action={
            <Link href="/instructor/courses/new">
              <Button>Create course</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {courses.map((course) => (
            <Card key={course.id} className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <p className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{course.title || "Untitled course"}</p>
                  <Badge variant={STATUS_VARIANT[course.status]}>{course.status.replace("_", " ")}</Badge>
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
                  {course.modules.length} modules &middot; Created {formatDate(course.createdAt)}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span className="font-mono text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">
                  {formatCurrency(course.price, course.currency)}
                </span>
                <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => removeCourse(course.id)}>
                  Delete
                </Button>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { DollarSign, BookOpen, Users, Star, PlusCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { useInstructorCourseStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, Card } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency } from "@saachi/utils";

export default function InstructorDashboardPage() {
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const { courses } = useInstructorCourseStore();

  const totalRevenue = courses.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.revenue, 0);
  const totalEnrollments = courses.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.enrollmentCount, 0);
  const avgRating = courses.length
    ? (courses.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.rating, 0) / courses.length).toFixed(1)
    : "Ã¢â‚¬â€";
  const published = courses.filter((c) => c.status === "published").length;

  const stats = [
    { label: "Total earnings", value: formatCurrency(totalRevenue, "USD"), icon: DollarSign },
    { label: "Published courses", value: String(published), icon: BookOpen },
    { label: "Total students", value: totalEnrollments.toLocaleString(), icon: Users },
    { label: "Average rating", value: avgRating, icon: Star }
  ];

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1100px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-8 flex flex-col items-start justify-between gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">
            Welcome back{session?.fullName ? `, ${session.fullName.split(" ")[0]}` : ""}
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Here's how your courses are performing.</p>
        </div>
        <Link href="/instructor/courses/new">
          <Button>
            <PlusCircle size={16} /> Create course
          </Button>
        </Link>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-10 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {stats.map((stat) => (
          <Card key={stat.label} className="p-5">
            <div className="mb-3 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control bg-teal-light text-teal">
              <stat.icon size={17} />
            </div>
            <div className="font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{stat.value}</div>
            <div className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{stat.label}</div>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      <h2 className="mb-4 font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">Your courses</h2>
      {courses.length === 0 ? (
        <Card className="p-6 text-center">
          <p className="mb-4 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
            You haven't created any courses yet. This is demo data - courses you create here are
            stored locally in your browser.
          </p>
          <Link href="/instructor/courses/new">
            <Button>Create your first course</Button>
          </Link>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {courses.slice(0, 5).map((course) => (
            <Card key={course.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4">
              <div>
                <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{course.title || "Untitled course"}</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] capitalize text-ink-soft">{course.status.replace("_", " ")}</p>
              </div>
              <span className="font-mono text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">
                {formatCurrency(course.revenue, course.currency)}
              </span>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\instructor\mentorship\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Users2, Plus, X } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Checkbox, Input } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency } from "@saachi/utils";

const DAYS = ["Mon", "Tue", "Wed", "Thu", "Fri", "Sat", "Sun"];

export default function InstructorMentorshipSettingsPage() {
  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(false);
  const [rate, setRate] = useState(40);
  const [slots, setSlots] = useState<string[]>(["Mon 3:00 PM", "Wed 11:00 AM"]);
  const [newSlot, setNewSlot] = useState("");
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);

  const addSlot = () => {
    if (!newSlot.trim()) return;
    setSlots((prev) => [...prev, newSlot.trim()]);
    setNewSlot("");
  };

  const removeSlot = (slot: string) => {
    setSlots((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s !== slot));
  };

  const handleSave = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSaved(true);
    setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 2500);
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[720px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control bg-teal-light text-teal">
          <Users2 size={18} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-display text-[24px] font-semibold text-ink">Mentorship Settings</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Offer paid 1-on-1 sessions to your students.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="flex flex-col gap-6">
        <Card className="p-6">
          <Checkbox
            label="Enable mentorship bookings for my profile"
            checked={enabled}
            onChange={(e) => setEnabled(e.target.checked)}
          />
        </Card>

        <Card className="p-6">
          <Input
            label="Hourly rate (USD)"
            type="number"
            min={0}
            value={rate}
            onChange={(e) => setRate(Number(e.target.value))}
            helperText={`Learners will see this shown as ${formatCurrency(rate, "USD")} / 30 min session.`}
          />
        </Card>

        <Card className="p-6">
          <h2 className="mb-4 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Available time slots</h2>
          <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {slots.map((slot) => (
              <span
                key={slot}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-line-strong px-3 py-1.5 text-[12px] text-ink"
              >
                {slot}
                <button type="button" onClick={() => removeSlot(slot)} className="text-ink-soft hover:text-error">
                  <X size={12} />
                </button>
              </span>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Input
              placeholder="e.g. Tue 4:00 PM"
              value={newSlot}
              onChange={(e) => setNewSlot(e.target.value)}
              className="h-10 flex-1"
            />
            <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={addSlot}>
              <Plus size={14} /> Add slot
            </Button>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-3 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
            Days for reference: {DAYS.join(", ")}
          </p>
        </Card>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Button type="submit">Save mentorship settings</Button>
          {saved && <span className="text-[13px] text-teal">Saved</span>}
        </div>
      </form>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\instructor\promotions\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Rocket, Check, Crown } from "lucide-react";
import { useInstructorCourseStore, usePromotionStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, Card, Select, Badge, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const TIERS = [
  {
    id: "bronze" as const,
    name: "Bronze",
    price: 15,
    features: ["Category page pinning", "14-day boost"]
  },
  {
    id: "silver" as const,
    name: "Silver",
    price: 35,
    features: ["Everything in Bronze", "Top-of-search placement", "14-day boost"]
  },
  {
    id: "gold" as const,
    name: "Gold",
    price: 75,
    features: ["Everything in Silver", "Homepage featured slot", "14-day boost", "Priority support"]
  }
];

export default function InstructorPromotionsPage() {
  const { courses } = useInstructorCourseStore();
  const promotions = usePromotionStore();
  const [selectedCourseId, setSelectedCourseId] = useState(courses[0]?.id ?? "");

  const courseOptions = courses.map((c) => ({ label: c.title || "Untitled course", value: c.id }));

  const handlePurchase = (tier: (typeof TIERS)[number]) => {
    const course = courses.find((c) => c.id === selectedCourseId);
    if (!course) return;
    promotions.purchase(course.id, course.title, tier.id);
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Promotions</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Boost a course's visibility across the marketplace.</p>
      </div>

      {courses.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<Rocket size={22} />}
          title="No courses to promote yet"
          description="Create a course first, then come back here to boost it."
        />
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="mb-6 max-w-[360px]">
            <Select
              label="Course to promote"
              options={courseOptions}
              value={selectedCourseId}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedCourseId(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-3">
            {TIERS.map((tier) => (
              <Card key={tier.id} className={tier.id === "gold" ? "border-2 border-ink p-6" : "p-6"}>
                {tier.id === "gold" && (
                  <span className="mb-3 flex w-fit items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-gold px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.05em] text-ink">
                    <Crown size={12} /> Best value
                  </span>
                )}
                <h3 className="text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">{tier.name}</h3>
                <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[24px] font-semibold text-ink">
                  {formatCurrency(tier.price, "USD")}
                </p>
                <ul className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-2">
                  {tier.features.map((f) => (
                    <li key={f} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
                      <Check size={14} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-teal" />
                      {f}
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
                <Button
                  className="mt-5 w-full"
                  variant={tier.id === "gold" ? "primary" : "secondary"}
                  onClick={() => handlePurchase(tier)}
                  disabled={!selectedCourseId}
                >
                  Boost with {tier.name}
                </Button>
              </Card>
            ))}
          </div>

          {promotions.active.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-10">
              <h2 className="mb-4 text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">Active promotions</h2>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
                {promotions.active.map((p) => (
                  <Card key={p.courseId} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4">
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{p.courseTitle}</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
                        Expires {formatDate(p.expiresAt)}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <Badge variant="gold">{p.tier}</Badge>
                  </Card>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { ReviewCard } from "@saachi/ui";
import { DUMMY_COURSES } from "@saachi/api-client";

export default function InstructorReviewsPage() {
  const allReviews = DUMMY_COURSES.flatMap((c) =>
    (c.reviews ?? []).map((r) => ({ ...r, courseTitle: c.title }))
  );

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[900px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <h1 className="mb-8 font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Reviews</h1>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        {allReviews.map((review) => (
          <div key={review.id}>
            <p className="mb-2 text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.04em] text-gold-dark">
              {review.courseTitle}
            </p>
            <ReviewCard {...review} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
