"use client";

import { Card, Input, Badge } from "@saachi/ui";
import { Search } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";

const DEMO_STUDENTS = [
  { id: "std_1", name: "Bilal Ahmed", email: "bilal@example.com", course: "Prompt Engineering Fundamentals", progress: 80 },
  { id: "std_2", name: "Hina Malik", email: "hina@example.com", course: "Prompt Engineering Fundamentals", progress: 45 },
  { id: "std_3", name: "Omar Sheikh", email: "omar@example.com", course: "AI-Powered Marketing Campaigns", progress: 100 },
  { id: "std_4", name: "Fatima Noor", email: "fatima@example.com", course: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI", progress: 20 }
];

export default function InstructorStudentsPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");

  const filtered = DEMO_STUDENTS.filter(
    (s) => s.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || s.course.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Students</h1>

      <div className="relative mb-6 max-w-[360px]">
        <Search size={16} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft/60" />
        <Input placeholder="Search students or courses" value={search} onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)} className="pl-10" />
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        {filtered.map((s) => (
          <Card key={s.id} className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{s.name}</p>
              <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{s.email}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{s.course}</p>
            </div>
            <Badge variant={s.progress === 100 ? "success" : "default"}>{s.progress}% complete</Badge>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { ShieldCheck, Upload, Clock, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Input, Badge } from "@saachi/ui";

type VerificationStatus = "not_submitted" | "pending" | "verified";

const TIER_STEPS = [
  { tier: "New Instructor", requirement: "Just approved, 0-2 published courses" },
  { tier: "Verified Instructor", requirement: "ID verified, 3+ courses, 4.0+ average rating" },
  { tier: "Top-Rated Instructor", requirement: "10+ courses, 4.5+ rating, consistent sales" },
  { tier: "Partner Instructor", requirement: "Invited/negotiated status" }
];

export default function VerificationStatusPage() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<VerificationStatus>("not_submitted");
  const [idFile, setIdFile] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [portfolioLink, setPortfolioLink] = useState("");

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setStatus("pending");
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[720px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control bg-teal-light text-teal">
          <ShieldCheck size={18} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-display text-[24px] font-semibold text-ink">Verification Status</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Get verified to unlock lower commission rates.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Card className="mb-6 p-6">
        {status === "not_submitted" && (
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="mb-1.5 block text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">
                Government ID or business registration
              </label>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIdFile("id-document-demo.pdf")}
                className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-control border border-dashed border-line-strong py-6 text-[13px] text-ink-soft hover:border-ink"
              >
                <Upload size={16} />
                {idFile ? idFile : "Click to upload (demo)"}
              </button>
            </div>
            <Input
              label="Portfolio / proof-of-work link"
              placeholder="GitHub, LinkedIn, or personal site"
              value={portfolioLink}
              onChange={(e) => setPortfolioLink(e.target.value)}
            />
            <Button type="submit" disabled={!idFile}>
              Submit for verification
            </Button>
          </form>
        )}

        {status === "pending" && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <Clock size={20} className="text-gold-dark" />
            <div>
              <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Under review</p>
              <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
                Your documents were submitted (demo). Admin review typically takes 2-3 business days.
              </p>
            </div>
            <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" className="ml-auto" onClick={() => setStatus("verified")}>
              Simulate approval
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}

        {status === "verified" && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <CheckCircle2 size={20} className="text-teal" />
            <div>
              <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Verified Instructor</p>
              <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
                You now qualify for a reduced commission rate and the Verified badge.
              </p>
            </div>
            <Badge variant="success" className="ml-auto">
              Verified
            </Badge>
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>

      <h2 className="mb-4 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Instructor trust tiers</h2>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        {TIER_STEPS.map((t) => (
          <Card key={t.tier} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">{t.tier}</p>
              <p className="mt-0.5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{t.requirement}</p>
            </div>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\_components\InstructorShell.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  BookOpen,
  PlusCircle,
  BarChart3,
  Wallet,
  Star,
  Users,
  Settings,
  LogOut,
  Menu,
  X,
  Rocket,
  Users2,
  ShieldCheck
} from "lucide-react";
import { useSession, useLogout } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { cn } from "@saachi/ui";

const NAV_ITEMS = [
  { label: "Dashboard", href: "/instructor/dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { label: "My Courses", href: "/instructor/courses", icon: BookOpen },
  { label: "Create Course", href: "/instructor/courses/new", icon: PlusCircle },
  { label: "Analytics", href: "/instructor/analytics", icon: BarChart3 },
  { label: "Earnings & Payouts", href: "/instructor/earnings", icon: Wallet },
  { label: "Promotions", href: "/instructor/promotions", icon: Rocket },
  { label: "Mentorship", href: "/instructor/mentorship", icon: Users2 },
  { label: "Reviews", href: "/instructor/reviews", icon: Star },
  { label: "Students", href: "/instructor/students", icon: Users },
  { label: "Verification", href: "/instructor/verification", icon: ShieldCheck },
  { label: "Settings", href: "/instructor/settings", icon: Settings }
];

export function InstructorShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const logout = useLogout();
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await logout.mutateAsync();
    router.push("/");
  };

  const sidebarContent = (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-6 py-6">
        <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-ink bg-teal font-display text-[15px] font-bold text-white">
          S
        </span>
        <div>
          <div className="font-display text-[16px] font-semibold leading-none text-ink">Saachi AI</div>
          <div className="mt-1 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-teal">Instructor</div>
        </div>
      </Link>

      <nav className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1 px-3">
        {NAV_ITEMS.map((item) => {
          const active = pathname === item.href || Boolean(pathname && pathname.startsWith(`${item.href}/`));
          return (
            <Link
              key={item.href}
              href={item.href}
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
              className={cn(
                "flex items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-3.5 py-2.5 text-[14px] font-medium transition-colors duration-150 ease-out",
                active ? "bg-teal-light text-teal" : "text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
              )}
            >
              <item.icon size={18} />
              {item.label}
            </Link>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      <div className="border-t border-line px-3 py-4">
        <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-3 px-3">
          <div className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-teal-light text-[13px] font-semibold text-teal">
            {session?.avatarUrl ? (
              // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
              <img src={session.avatarUrl} alt={session.fullName} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
            ) : (
              session?.fullName?.charAt(0) ?? "?"
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="truncate text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">{session?.fullName ?? "Guest"}</div>
            <div className="truncate text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{session?.email ?? ""}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Link
          href="/dashboard"
          className="mb-1 flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-3.5 py-2.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
        >
          Switch to learner view
        </Link>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleLogout}
          disabled={logout.isPending}
          className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-3.5 py-2.5 text-[14px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error"
        >
          <LogOut size={18} />
          {logout.isPending ? "Logging out..." : "Log out"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-paper">
      <div className="hidden w-[260px] shrink-0 border-r border-line bg-card md:fixed md:inset-y-0 md:flex">
        {sidebarContent}
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line bg-card px-4 py-3 md:hidden">
        <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-ink bg-teal font-display text-[13px] font-bold text-white">
            S
          </span>
          <span className="font-display text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Saachi AI Instructor</span>
        </Link>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setMobileOpen(true)}
          aria-label="Open menu"
          className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink"
        >
          <Menu size={20} />
        </button>
      </div>

      {mobileOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex md:hidden">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-ink/40" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)} />
          <div className="relative flex h-full w-[280px] flex-col bg-card">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
              aria-label="Close menu"
              className="absolute right-3 top-3 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink"
            >
              <X size={20} />
            </button>
            {sidebarContent}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="md:pl-[260px]">{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\cart\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ShoppingCart, X, Tag } from "lucide-react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useCartStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, Card, Input, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency } from "@saachi/utils";

export default function CartPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const cart = useCartStore();
  const [couponInput, setCouponInput] = useState("");
  const [couponMessage, setCouponMessage] = useState<{ text: string; ok: boolean } | null>(null);

  const currency = cart.items[0]?.currency ?? "USD";

  const handleApplyCoupon = () => {
    if (!couponInput.trim()) return;
    const result = cart.applyCoupon(couponInput);
    setCouponMessage({ text: result.message, ok: result.success });
  };

  if (cart.items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
        <h1 className="mb-8 font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Your cart</h1>
        <EmptyState
          icon={<ShoppingCart size={22} />}
          title="Your cart is empty"
          description="Browse the marketplace and add a course to get started."
          action={
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button>Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <h1 className="mb-8 font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">
        Your cart <span className="text-ink-soft">({cart.items.length})</span>
      </h1>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_320px]">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {cart.items.map((item) => (
            <Card key={item.courseId} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
              <Link href={`/courses/${item.slug}`} className="block h-[90px] w-[160px] shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-control bg-paper-dim">
                {item.thumbnailUrl && (
                  // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                  <img src={item.thumbnailUrl} alt={item.title} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
                )}
              </Link>
              <div className="flex-1">
                <Link href={`/courses/${item.slug}`} className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink hover:text-gold-dark">
                  {item.title}
                </Link>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{item.instructorName}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-4">
                <span className="font-mono text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">
                  {formatCurrency(item.price, item.currency)}
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => cart.removeItem(item.courseId)}
                  aria-label="Remove from cart"
                  className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-ink-soft transition-colors hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error"
                >
                  <X size={16} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="lg:sticky lg:top-6 lg:self-start">
          <Card className="p-6">
            <h2 className="mb-4 text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">Order summary</h2>

            <div className="mb-4 flex flex-col gap-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Tag size={15} className="text-ink-soft" />
                <Input
                  placeholder="Coupon code"
                  value={couponInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setCouponInput(e.target.value)}
                  className="h-10"
                />
                <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={handleApplyCoupon}>
                  Apply
                </Button>
              </div>
              {couponMessage && (
                <p className={couponMessage.ok ? "text-[12px] text-teal" : "text-[12px] text-error"}>
                  {couponMessage.text}
                </p>
              )}
              {cart.coupon && (
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
                  <span>Applied: {cart.coupon.code}</span>
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => cart.removeCoupon()} className="text-error hover:underline">
                    Remove
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 border-t border-line pt-4 text-[14px]">
              <div className="flex justify-between text-ink-soft">
                <span>Subtotal</span>
                <span>{formatCurrency(cart.subtotal(), currency)}</span>
              </div>
              {cart.coupon && (
                <div className="flex justify-between text-teal">
                  <span>Discount ({cart.coupon.percentOff}%)</span>
                  <span>-{formatCurrency(cart.discount(), currency)}</span>
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="flex justify-between border-t border-line pt-2 text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">
                <span>Total</span>
                <span>{formatCurrency(cart.total(), currency)}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <Button size="lg" className="mt-6 w-full" onClick={() => router.push("/checkout")}>
              Proceed to checkout
            </Button>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\certificates\page.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { Award, Download, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { useEnrollmentStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

function verificationId(courseId: string) {
  return `SAACHI-${courseId.toUpperCase().replace("CRS_", "")}-DEMO`;
}

export default function CertificatesPage() {
  const { enrollments } = useEnrollmentStore();
  const { data: session } = useSession();

  const completed = enrollments.filter((e) => e.progressPercent >= 100);

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Certificates</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Courses you've fully completed.</p>
      </div>

      {completed.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<Award size={22} />}
          title="No certificates yet"
          description="Complete every lesson in a course to earn your certificate."
          action={
            <Link href="/my-courses">
              <Button>Go to My Courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {completed.map((course) => (
            <Card key={course.courseId} className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-2 border-ink bg-gold-tint p-6">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <Award size={28} className="text-gold-dark" />
                <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.05em] text-ink-soft">
                  Certificate of Completion
                </span>
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">{course.title}</h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Awarded to {session?.fullName ?? "Learner"}</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-ink-soft/70">
                  Completed {formatDate(course.purchasedAt)}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-ink-soft">
                <ShieldCheck size={13} /> Verification ID: {verificationId(course.courseId)}
              </div>
              <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" className="mt-2 w-fit">
                <Download size={15} /> Download (demo)
              </Button>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";

export default function LearnerDashboardPage() {

  const { data, isLoading } = useCourseList({ sort: "popular" });

  const courses = data?.items ?? [];

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-10">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[28px] font-semibold text-ink">
          Welcome back{session?.fullName ? `, ${session.fullName.split(" ")[0]}` : ""}
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
          Pick up where you left off, or explore something new.
        </p>
      </div>

      <Card className="mb-10 flex flex-col items-start gap-4 p-6 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">This is demo data</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
            You are signed in with a local demo session - no backend or real account required yet.
          </p>
        </div>
        <Link href="/courses">
          <Button>Browse courses</Button>
        </Link>
      </Card>

      <h2 className="mb-4 font-display text-[20px] font-semibold text-ink">Recommended for you</h2>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2">
        {isLoading
          ? Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => <SkeletonCard key={i} />)
          : courses.slice(0, 4).map((course) => (
              <CourseCard
                key={course.id}
                slug={course.slug}
                title={course.title}
                thumbnailUrl={course.thumbnailUrl}
                instructorName={course.instructor.fullName}
                price={course.price}
                currency={course.currency}
                rating={course.rating}
                ratingCount={course.ratingCount}
              />
            ))}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Users2, Clock, Video, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency } from "@saachi/utils";

const MENTORS = [
  {
    id: "ins_003",
    name: "Ayesha Khan",
    expertise: "AI Automation & n8n Workflows",
    avatarSeed: "ayesha-khan-instructor",
    rate: 40,
    slots: ["Mon 3:00 PM", "Wed 11:00 AM", "Fri 4:30 PM"]
  },
  {
    id: "ins_004",
    name: "Bilal Ahmed",
    expertise: "AI Customer Support Systems",
    avatarSeed: "bilal-ahmed-instructor",
    rate: 55,
    slots: ["Tue 2:00 PM", "Thu 10:00 AM"]
  },
  {
    id: "ins_006",
    name: "Usman Ali",
    expertise: "AI API Integration for Developers",
    avatarSeed: "usman-ali",
    rate: 65,
    slots: ["Mon 6:00 PM", "Wed 7:00 PM", "Sat 12:00 PM"]
  }
];

export default function MentorshipPage() {
  const [booked, setBooked] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  const handleBook = (mentorId: string, slot: string) => {
    setBooked((prev) => ({ ...prev, [mentorId]: slot }));
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Mentorship</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
          Book 1-on-1 time with an instructor for personalized guidance.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        {MENTORS.map((mentor) => {
          const bookedSlot = booked[mentor.id];
          return (
            <Card key={mentor.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <div className="flex h-14 w-14 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-teal-light text-[16px] font-semibold text-teal">
                  {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
                  <img
                    src={`https://picsum.photos/seed/${mentor.avatarSeed}/150/150`}
                    alt={mentor.name}
                    className="h-full w-full object-cover"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{mentor.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{mentor.expertise}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
                    <Clock size={12} /> {formatCurrency(mentor.rate, "USD")} / 30 min session
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {bookedSlot ? (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-control bg-teal-light px-4 py-2.5 text-[13px] font-semibold text-teal">
                  <CheckCircle2 size={16} /> Booked for {bookedSlot}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  {mentor.slots.map((slot) => (
                    <button
                      key={slot}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleBook(mentor.id, slot)}
                      className="rounded-full border border-line-strong px-3.5 py-2 text-[12px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:border-ink hover:text-ink"
                    >
                      {slot}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </Card>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {Object.keys(booked).length > 0 && (
        <Card className="mt-8 flex items-center gap-3 p-5">
          <Video size={18} className="shrink-0 text-teal" />
          <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
            This is a demo booking - no real calendar invite or payment was processed. Once the
            backend Mentorship module (Doc 1, Section 6.2) is live, this will generate a real
            video-call link and checkout.
          </p>
        </Card>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\my-courses\page.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { BookOpen } from "lucide-react";
import { useEnrollmentStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { useCourseDetail } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

function ContinueButton({ courseId, slug }: { courseId: string; slug: string }) {
  const { data: course } = useCourseDetail(slug);
  const firstLessonId = course?.curriculum?.[0]?.lessons?.[0]?.id;

  if (!firstLessonId) {
    return (
      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" className="shrink-0" disabled>
        Loading...
      </Button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Link href={`/learn/${courseId}/${firstLessonId}`}>
      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" className="shrink-0">
        Continue
      </Button>
    </Link>
  );
}

export default function MyCoursesPage() {
  const { enrollments } = useEnrollmentStore();

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">My Courses</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Everything you're enrolled in, in one place.</p>
      </div>

      {enrollments.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<BookOpen size={22} />}
          title="No courses yet"
          description="Once you purchase a course, it will show up here with your progress."
          action={
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button>Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {enrollments.map((course) => (
            <Card key={course.courseId} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
              <Link href={`/courses/${course.slug}`} className="block h-[90px] w-[160px] shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-control bg-paper-dim">
                {course.thumbnailUrl && (
                  // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                  <img src={course.thumbnailUrl} alt={course.title} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
                )}
              </Link>
              <div className="flex-1">
                <Link href={`/courses/${course.slug}`} className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink hover:text-gold-dark">
                  {course.title}
                </Link>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{course.instructorName}</p>
                <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="h-2 w-full max-w-[220px] overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-paper-dim">
                    <div
                      className="h-full rounded-full bg-gold"
                      style={{ width: `${course.progressPercent}%` }}
                    />
                  </div>
                  <span className="shrink-0 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{course.progressPercent}% complete</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              {course.progressPercent >= 100 ? (
                <Link href="/certificates">
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" className="shrink-0">
                    View certificate
                  </Button>
                </Link>
              ) : (
                <ContinueButton courseId={course.courseId} slug={course.slug} />
              )}
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\notifications\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { Bell, BookOpen, MessageSquare, CreditCard, Info } from "lucide-react";
import { useNotificationStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, Card, EmptyState, Badge } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatRelativeTime } from "@saachi/utils";

const TYPE_ICON = {
  course: BookOpen,
  system: Info,
  community: MessageSquare,
  payment: CreditCard
} as const;

export default function NotificationsPage() {
  const { items, markAllRead, markRead } = useNotificationStore();
  const unreadCount = items.filter((n) => !n.read).length;

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[800px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Notifications</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
            {unreadCount > 0 ? `${unreadCount} unread` : "You're all caught up"}
          </p>
        </div>
        {unreadCount > 0 && (
          <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={markAllRead}>
            Mark all as read
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>

      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<Bell size={22} />} title="No notifications" description="Nothing here yet." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {items.map((n) => {
            const Icon = TYPE_ICON[n.type];
            return (
              <Card
                key={n.id}
                onClick={() => markRead(n.id)}
                className={[
                  "flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-4 p-4",
                  !n.read ? "border-gold-dark/40 bg-gold-tint/40" : ""
                ].join(" ")}
              >
                <div className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-teal-light text-teal">
                  <Icon size={16} />
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <h3 className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{n.title}</h3>
                    {!n.read && <Badge variant="gold">New</Badge>}
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{n.body}</p>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-[12px] text-ink-soft/60">{formatRelativeTime(n.createdAt)}</p>
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { Receipt, Download } from "lucide-react";
import { useOrderStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, Card, Badge, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

export default function OrdersPage() {
  const { orders } = useOrderStore();

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[900px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Order History</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">All your past purchases on Saachi AI.</p>
      </div>

      {orders.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<Receipt size={22} />}
          title="No orders yet"
          description="Once you complete a purchase, it will show up here."
          action={
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button>Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {orders.map((order) => (
            <Card key={order.id} className="p-5">
              <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{order.id}</p>
                  <p className="mt-0.5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{formatDate(order.createdAt)}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <Badge variant={order.status === "paid" ? "success" : "error"}>
                    {order.status === "paid" ? "Paid" : "Refunded"}
                  </Badge>
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary">
                    <Download size={14} /> Invoice
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 border-t border-line pt-3">
                {order.items.map((item) => (
                  <div key={item.courseId} className="flex justify-between text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
                    <span className="line-clamp-1 pr-2">{item.title}</span>
                    <span className="shrink-0 text-ink">{formatCurrency(item.price, order.currency)}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 flex justify-between border-t border-line pt-3 text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">
                <span>Total ({order.paymentMethod.replace("_", " ")})</span>
                <span>{formatCurrency(order.total, order.currency)}</span>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Input, Textarea } from "@saachi/ui";

const SETTINGS_TABS = [
  { label: "Profile", href: "/settings/profile" },
  { label: "Security", href: "/settings/security" }
];

export default function SettingsProfilePage() {
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState(session?.fullName ?? "");
  const [bio, setBio] = useState("");
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);

  const handleSave = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    // NOTE: demo-only, local state. Replace with Users API PATCH once backend exists.
    setSaved(true);
    setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 2500);
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[720px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Settings</h1>

      <div className="mb-8 flex gap-2 border-b border-line">
        {SETTINGS_TABS.map((tab) => (
          <Link
            key={tab.href}
            href={tab.href}
            className="border-b-2 border-ink px-1 pb-3 text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink"
          >
            {tab.label}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>

      <Card className="p-6">
        <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-teal-light text-[20px] font-semibold text-teal">
            {session?.avatarUrl ? (
              // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
              <img src={session.avatarUrl} alt={session.fullName} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
            ) : (
              session?.fullName?.charAt(0) ?? "?"
            )}
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Profile photo</p>
            <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">Demo avatar - upload not wired to a backend yet.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Full name" value={fullName} onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)} />
          <Input label="Email" value={session?.email ?? ""} disabled />
          <Textarea
            label="Bio"
            placeholder="Tell us a bit about yourself..."
            value={bio}
            onChange={(e) => setBio(e.target.value)}
          />
          <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-3">
            <Button type="submit">Save changes</Button>
            {saved && <span className="text-[13px] text-teal">Saved</span>}
          </div>
        </form>
      </Card>
    </main>
  );
}

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <ShieldCheck size={20} className="text-teal" />
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Two-factor authentication</h2>

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\wishlist\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { Heart } from "lucide-react";
import { useWishlistStore, useCartStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, Card, EmptyState, CourseCard } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function WishlistPage() {
  const wishlist = useWishlistStore();
  const cart = useCartStore();

  const moveToCart = (item: (typeof wishlist.items)[number]) => {
    cart.addItem({
      courseId: item.courseId,
      slug: item.slug,
      title: item.title,
      thumbnailUrl: item.thumbnailUrl,
      instructorName: item.instructorName,
      price: item.price,
      currency: item.currency
    });
    wishlist.remove(item.courseId);
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-6 py-10 md:px-10">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-semibold text-ink">Wishlist</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Courses you've saved for later.</p>
      </div>

      {wishlist.items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<Heart size={22} />}
          title="Your wishlist is empty"
          description="Save courses you're interested in and come back to them anytime."
          action={
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button>Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {wishlist.items.map((item) => (
            <Card key={item.courseId} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
              <Link href={`/courses/${item.slug}`} className="block h-[90px] w-[160px] shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-control bg-paper-dim">
                {item.thumbnailUrl && (
                  // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                  <img src={item.thumbnailUrl} alt={item.title} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
                )}
              </Link>
              <div className="flex-1">
                <Link href={`/courses/${item.slug}`} className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink hover:text-gold-dark">
                  {item.title}
                </Link>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{item.instructorName}</p>
                <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
                  {new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: item.currency }).format(item.price)}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex shrink-0 gap-2">
                <Button size="sm" onClick={() => moveToCart(item)}>
                  Add to cart
                </Button>
                <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => wishlist.remove(item.courseId)}>
                  Remove
                </Button>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\_components\LearnerShell.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  BookOpen,
  Heart,
  ShoppingCart,
  Award,
  Bell,
  Settings,
  LogOut,
  Menu,
  X,
  Receipt,
  Users2
} from "lucide-react";
import { useSession, useLogout } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { useCartStore, useNotificationStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { useRealtimeNotifications } from "../../../hooks/useRealtimeNotifications";
import { cn } from "@saachi/ui";

const NAV_ITEMS = [
  { label: "Dashboard", href: "/dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { label: "My Courses", href: "/my-courses", icon: BookOpen },
  { label: "Wishlist", href: "/wishlist", icon: Heart },
  { label: "Cart", href: "/cart", icon: ShoppingCart },
  { label: "Certificates", href: "/certificates", icon: Award },
  { label: "Orders", href: "/orders", icon: Receipt },
  { label: "Mentorship", href: "/mentorship", icon: Users2 },
  { label: "Notifications", href: "/notifications", icon: Bell },
  { label: "Settings", href: "/settings/profile", icon: Settings }
];

export function LearnerShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const logout = useLogout();
  const cartCount = useCartStore((s) => s.items.length);
  const unreadCount = useNotificationStore((s) => s.items.filter((n) => !n.read).length);
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);

  useRealtimeNotifications(true);

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await logout.mutateAsync();
    router.push("/");
  };

  const sidebarContent = (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-6 py-6">
        <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-ink bg-gold font-display text-[15px] font-bold text-ink">
          S
        </span>
        <span className="font-display text-[17px] font-semibold text-ink">Saachi AI</span>
      </Link>

      <nav className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1 px-3">
        {NAV_ITEMS.map((item) => {
          const active = pathname === item.href || Boolean(pathname && pathname.startsWith(`${item.href}/`));
          return (
            <Link
              key={item.href}
              href={item.href}
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
              className={cn(
                "flex items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-3.5 py-2.5 text-[14px] font-medium transition-colors duration-150 ease-out",
                active ? "bg-gold-tint text-ink" : "text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
              )}
            >
              <item.icon size={18} />
              {item.label}
              {item.href === "/cart" && cartCount > 0 && (
                <span className="ml-auto flex h-5 min-w-[20px] items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold px-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-ink">
                  {cartCount}
                </span>
              )}
              {item.href === "/notifications" && unreadCount > 0 && (
                <span className="ml-auto flex h-5 min-w-[20px] items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-error px-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-white">
                  {unreadCount}
                </span>
              )}
            </Link>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      <div className="border-t border-line px-3 py-4">
        <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-3 px-3">
          <div className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-teal-light text-[13px] font-semibold text-teal">
            {session?.avatarUrl ? (
              // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
              <img src={session.avatarUrl} alt={session.fullName} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
            ) : (
              session?.fullName?.charAt(0) ?? "?"
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="truncate text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">{session?.fullName ?? "Guest"}</div>
            <div className="truncate text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{session?.email ?? ""}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleLogout}
          disabled={logout.isPending}
          className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-3.5 py-2.5 text-[14px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error"
        >
          <LogOut size={18} />
          {logout.isPending ? "Logging out..." : "Log out"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-paper">
      <div className="hidden w-[260px] shrink-0 border-r border-line bg-card md:fixed md:inset-y-0 md:flex">
        {sidebarContent}
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line bg-card px-4 py-3 md:hidden">
        <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-ink bg-gold font-display text-[13px] font-bold text-ink">
            S
          </span>
          <span className="font-display text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Saachi AI</span>
        </Link>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setMobileOpen(true)}
          aria-label="Open menu"
          className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink"
        >
          <Menu size={20} />
        </button>
      </div>

      {mobileOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex md:hidden">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-ink/40" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)} />
          <div className="relative flex h-full w-[280px] flex-col bg-card">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
              aria-label="Close menu"
              className="absolute right-3 top-3 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink"
            >
              <X size={20} />
            </button>
            {sidebarContent}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="md:pl-[260px]">{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Search } from "lucide-react";
import { useCourseList } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Input, Select, EmptyState, SkeletonCard, CourseCard, FilterSidebar } from "@saachi/ui";
import type { FilterSidebarValue } from "@saachi/ui";
import { Navbar } from "../_components/Navbar";
import { Footer } from "../_components/Footer";

const CATEGORY_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "AI Fundamentals & Prompt Engineering", value: "ai-fundamentals" },
  { label: "AI for Marketing & Sales", value: "ai-marketing" },
  { label: "AI Automation & Workflow Tools", value: "ai-automation" },
  { label: "AI Customer Support & Communication", value: "ai-support" },
  { label: "AI Website & App Development", value: "ai-dev" },
  { label: "AI Integration for Developers", value: "ai-integration" }
];

const LEVEL_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Beginner", value: "beginner" },
  { label: "Intermediate", value: "intermediate" },
  { label: "Advanced", value: "advanced" }
];

const LANGUAGE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "English", value: "en" },
  { label: "Urdu", value: "ur" }
];

const SORT_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Most popular", value: "popular" },
  { label: "Newest", value: "newest" },
  { label: "Price: Low to High", value: "price_asc" },
  { label: "Price: High to Low", value: "price_desc" },
  { label: "Highest rated", value: "rating" }
];

export default function CourseMarketplacePage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState("popular");
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<FilterSidebarValue>({
    categories: [],
    level: undefined,
    language: undefined
  });

  const queryFilters = useMemo(
    () => ({
      search: search || undefined,
      category: filters.categories[0],
      level: filters.level,
      language: filters.language,
      sort
    }),
    [search, filters, sort]
  );

  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useCourseList(queryFilters);

  const courses = data?.items ?? [];

  return (
    <>
      <Navbar />
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1140px] px-6 py-16">
        <div className="mb-10">
          <h1 className="font-display text-[32px] font-semibold text-ink">Explore courses</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-[15px] text-ink-soft">
            Practical AI skills, taught by practitioners who use them every day.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-8 flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
          <div className="relative flex-1">
            <Search
              size={18}
              className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft/60"
            />
            <Input
              placeholder="Search courses, e.g. 'n8n automation'"
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="sm:w-[240px]">
            <Select options={SORT_OPTIONS} value={sort} onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[260px_1fr]">
          <FilterSidebar
            categoryOptions={CATEGORY_OPTIONS}
            levelOptions={LEVEL_OPTIONS}
            languageOptions={LANGUAGE_OPTIONS}
            value={filters}
            onChange={setFilters}
          />

          <section>
            {isLoading && (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
                {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => (
                  <SkeletonCard key={i} />
                ))}
              </div>
            )}

            {isError && (
              <EmptyState
                title="Couldn't load courses"
                description="Something went wrong while fetching the course list. Please try again."
              />
            )}

            {!isLoading && !isError && courses.length === 0 && (
              <EmptyState
                title="No courses found"
                description="Try adjusting your filters or search terms."
              />
            )}

            {!isLoading && !isError && courses.length > 0 && (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
                {courses.map((course) => (
                  <CourseCard
                    key={course.id}
                    slug={course.slug}
                    title={course.title}
                    thumbnailUrl={course.thumbnailUrl}
                    instructorName={course.instructor.fullName}
                    price={course.price}
                    currency={course.currency}
                    rating={course.rating}
                    ratingCount={course.ratingCount}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </section>
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(marketing)\search\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Search } from "lucide-react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { DUMMY_COURSES } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { CourseCard, Input, EmptyState, Card } from "@saachi/ui";
import { Navbar } from "../_components/Navbar";
import { Footer } from "../_components/Footer";

function slugifyName(name: string) {
  return name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/(^-|-$)/g, "");
}

export default function SearchPage() {
  const params = useSearchParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const initialQuery = params.get("q") ?? "";
  const [query, setQuery] = useState(initialQuery);

  const results = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return { courses: [], instructors: [] };

    const courses = DUMMY_COURSES.filter((c) => c.title.toLowerCase().includes(q));

    const instructorMap = new Map<string, { name: string; slug: string; avatarUrl?: string; courseCount: number }>();
    DUMMY_COURSES.forEach((c) => {
      if (c.instructor.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(q)) {
        const slug = slugifyName(c.instructor.fullName);
        const existing = instructorMap.get(slug);
        if (existing) {
          existing.courseCount += 1;
        } else {
          instructorMap.set(slug, {
            name: c.instructor.fullName,
            slug,
            avatarUrl: c.instructor.avatarUrl,
            courseCount: 1
          });
        }
      }
    });

    return { courses, instructors: Array.from(instructorMap.values()) };
  }, [query]);

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    router.push(`/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`);
  };

  const hasResults = results.courses.length > 0 || results.instructors.length > 0;

  return (
    <>
      <Navbar />
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-6 py-16">
        <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[28px] font-semibold text-ink">Search</h1>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="relative mb-10 max-w-[520px]">
          <Search size={18} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft/60" />
          <Input
            placeholder="Search courses and instructors"
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="pl-10"
          />
        </form>

        {!query.trim() ? (
          <EmptyState title="Start typing to search" description="Search across all courses and instructors." />
        ) : !hasResults ? (
          <EmptyState title="No results found" description={`Nothing matched "${query}". Try a different term.`} />
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-14">
            {results.instructors.length > 0 && (
              <div>
                <h2 className="mb-4 text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">Instructors</h2>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                  {results.instructors.map((instructor) => (
                    <Link key={instructor.slug} href={`/instructors/${instructor.slug}`}>
                      <Card className="flex items-center gap-4 p-4">
                        <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-teal-light text-[15px] font-semibold text-teal">
                          {instructor.avatarUrl ? (
                            // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                            <img src={instructor.avatarUrl} alt={instructor.name} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
                          ) : (
                            instructor.name.charAt(0)
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{instructor.name}</p>
                          <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{instructor.courseCount} courses</p>
                        </div>
                      </Card>
                    </Link>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {results.courses.length > 0 && (
              <div>
                <h2 className="mb-4 text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">Courses</h2>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
                  {results.courses.map((course) => (
                    <CourseCard
                      key={course.id}
                      slug={course.slug}
                      title={course.title}
                      thumbnailUrl={course.thumbnailUrl}
                      instructorName={course.instructor.fullName}
                      price={course.price}
                      currency={course.currency}
                      rating={course.rating}
                      ratingCount={course.ratingCount}
                    />
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(marketing)\_components\LocaleSwitcher.tsx

"use client";

import { Languages } from "lucide-react";
import { useLocaleStore } from "@saachi/store";

export function LocaleSwitcher() {
  const { locale, setLocale } = useLocaleStore();

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => setLocale(locale === "en" ? "ur" : "en")}
      className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-line-strong px-3 py-1.5 text-[12px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:border-ink hover:text-ink"
      aria-label="Switch language"
      title={locale === "en" ? "Switch to Urdu" : "Switch to English"}
    >
      <Languages size={13} />
      {locale === "en" ? "EN" : "UR"}
    </button>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(marketing)\_components\Navbar.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Menu, X, ShoppingCart, Search } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@saachi/ui";
import { useSession, useLogout } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { LocaleSwitcher } from "./LocaleSwitcher";

const NAV_LINKS = [
  { label: "Explore Courses", href: "/courses" },
  { label: "Become an Instructor", href: "/instructor/dashboard" },
  { label: "Pricing", href: "/pricing" },
  { label: "About", href: "/about" }
];

export function Navbar() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [scrolled, setScrolled] = useState(false);
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const logout = useLogout();

  useEffect(() => {
    const onScroll = () => setScrolled(window.scrollY > 24);
    onScroll();
    window.addEventListener("scroll", onScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", onScroll);
  }, []);

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await logout.mutateAsync();
    setMobileOpen(false);
    router.push("/");
  };

  return (
    <header
      className={[
        "sticky top-0 z-50 w-full border-b transition-colors duration-200 ease-out",
        scrolled ? "border-line bg-paper/95 backdrop-blur-sm" : "border-transparent bg-paper"
      ].join(" ")}
    >
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-[72px] max-w-[1140px] items-center justify-between px-6">
        <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-ink bg-gold font-display text-[15px] font-bold text-ink">
            S
          </span>
          <span className="font-display text-[18px] font-semibold tracking-[-0.01em] text-ink">
            Saachi AI
          </span>
        </Link>

        <nav className="hidden items-center gap-8 md:flex">
          {NAV_LINKS.map((link) => (
            <Link
              key={link.href}
              href={link.href}
              className="text-[14px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:text-ink"
            >
              {link.label}
            </Link>
          ))}
        </nav>

        <div className="hidden items-center gap-3 md:flex">
          <Link
            href="/search"
            aria-label="Search"
            className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
          >
            <Search size={18} />
          </Link>
          <LocaleSwitcher />
          {session ? (
            <>
              <Link
                href="/cart"
                aria-label="Cart"
                className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
              >
                <ShoppingCart size={19} />
              </Link>
              <Link
                href="/dashboard"
                className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-line-strong bg-card py-1.5 pl-1.5 pr-4 transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:border-ink"
              >
                <span className="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-teal-light text-[12px] font-semibold text-teal">
                  {session.avatarUrl ? (
                    // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                    <img src={session.avatarUrl} alt={session.fullName} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
                  ) : (
                    session.fullName.charAt(0)
                  )}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">{session.fullName.split(" ")[0]}</span>
              </Link>
              <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={handleLogout} disabled={logout.isPending}>
                {logout.isPending ? "..." : "Log out"}
              </Button>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Link href="/login">
                <Button variant="ghost" size="sm">
                  Log in
                </Button>
              </Link>
              <Link href="/register">
                <Button variant="primary" size="sm">
                  Get Started
                </Button>
              </Link>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setMobileOpen((v) => !v)}
          className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink md:hidden"
          aria-label="Toggle menu"
        >
          {mobileOpen ? <X size={22} /> : <Menu size={22} />}
        </button>
      </div>

      {mobileOpen && (
        <div className="border-t border-line bg-paper px-6 py-6 md:hidden">
          <nav className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            {NAV_LINKS.map((link) => (
              <Link
                key={link.href}
                href={link.href}
                onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
                className="text-[15px] font-medium text-ink"
              >
                {link.label}
              </Link>
            ))}
          </nav>
          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3">
            {session ? (
              <>
                <Link href="/dashboard" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}>
                  <Button variant="secondary" size="md" className="w-full">
                    Dashboard
                  </Button>
                </Link>
                <Link href="/cart" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}>
                  <Button variant="secondary" size="md" className="w-full">
                    Cart
                  </Button>
                </Link>
                <Button
                  variant="ghost"
                  size="md"
                  className="w-full"
                  onClick={handleLogout}
                  disabled={logout.isPending}
                >
                  {logout.isPending ? "Logging out..." : "Log out"}
                </Button>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Link href="/login" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}>
                  <Button variant="secondary" size="md" className="w-full">
                    Log in
                  </Button>
                </Link>
                <Link href="/register" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}>
                  <Button variant="primary" size="md" className="w-full">
                    Get Started
                  </Button>
                </Link>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </header>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\providers.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";

export function Providers({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [queryClient] = useState(
    () =>
      new QueryClient({
        defaultOptions: {
          queries: {
            staleTime: 30 * 1000,
            refetchOnWindowFocus: true,
            retry: 1
          }
        }
      })
  );

  return <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>;
}



================================================================================
FILE: apps\web\hooks\useRealtimeNotifications.ts
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useNotificationStore } from "@saachi/store";

const DEMO_MESSAGES = [
  { title: "New course recommendation", body: "Based on your activity, you might like AI Automation & Workflow Tools.", type: "system" as const },
  { title: "Instructor replied", body: "Your question in the course Q&A received a new reply.", type: "community" as const },
  { title: "Payment reminder", body: "A saved cart item has a limited-time coupon expiring soon.", type: "payment" as const }
];

export function useRealtimeNotifications(enabled: boolean) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const msg = DEMO_MESSAGES[Math.floor(Math.random() * DEMO_MESSAGES.length)];
      const store = useNotificationStore.getState();
      const alreadyExists = store.items.some((n) => n.title === msg.title && !n.read);
      if (alreadyExists) return;

      useNotificationStore.setState({
        items: [
          {
            id: `ntf_rt_${Date.now()}`,
            title: msg.title,
            body: msg.body,
            type: msg.type,
            createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
            read: false
          },
          ...store.items
        ]
      });
    }, 45000);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [enabled]);
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps\web\middleware.ts
================================================================================
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";

const LEARNER_PREFIX = "/dashboard";
const INSTRUCTOR_PREFIX = "/instructor";

export function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;
  const sessionCookie = request.cookies.get("saachi_session");

  const isProtected =
    pathname.startsWith(LEARNER_PREFIX) || pathname.startsWith(INSTRUCTOR_PREFIX);

  if (isProtected && !sessionCookie) {
    const loginUrl = new URL("/login", request.url);
    loginUrl.searchParams.set("redirect", pathname);
    return NextResponse.redirect(loginUrl);
  }

  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ["/dashboard/:path*", "/instructor/:path*"]
};

/** @type {import("next").NextConfig} */
const nextConfig = {
  reactStrictMode: true,
  transpilePackages: ["@saachi/ui", "@saachi/api-client", "@saachi/utils", "@saachi/store"],
  images: {
    remotePatterns: [
      { protocol: "https", hostname: "**.cloudfront.net" },
      { protocol: "https", hostname: "res.cloudinary.com" }
    ]
  },
  async rewrites() {
    return [
      {
        source: "/api/proxy/:path*",
        destination: `${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL}/:path*`
      }
    ];
  }
};

module.exports = nextConfig;

FILE: packages\api-client\src\hooks\useAdminCourses.ts

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  ADMIN_DUMMY_COURSES,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_CATEGORIES,
  type AdminCourse,
  type AdminCourseStatus,
  type AdminCategory
} from "../dummy-data-admin-courses";

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 350): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: demo-only, in-memory. Replace with real Admin-Courses / Categories API (Doc 3).
let COURSES_STATE: AdminCourse[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_COURSES];
let CATEGORIES_STATE: AdminCategory[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_CATEGORIES];

export const adminCourseKeys = {
  all: ["admin", "courses"] as const,
  moderationQueue: ["admin", "courses", "moderation-queue"] as const,
  catalog: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "courses", "catalog", filters] as const,
  categories: ["admin", "categories"] as const
};

export function useAdminModerationQueue() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminCourseKeys.moderationQueue,
    queryFn: async () => delay(COURSES_STATE.filter((c) => c.status === "pending_review"))
  });
}

export type AdminCourseCatalogFilters = {
  search?: string;
  categoryId?: string;
  status?: string;
};

export function useAdminCourseCatalog(filters: AdminCourseCatalogFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminCourseKeys.catalog(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...COURSES_STATE];
      if (filters.search) {
        const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        items = items.filter((c) => c.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.instructorName.toLowerCase().includes(q));
      }
      if (filters.categoryId) items = items.filter((c) => c.categoryId === filters.categoryId);
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((c) => c.status === filters.status);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useModerateCourse() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({
      id,
      decision,
      reason
    }: {
      id: string;
      decision: "published" | "rejected";
      reason?: string;
    }) => {
      COURSES_STATE = COURSES_STATE.map((c) =>
        c.id === id ? { ...c, status: decision as AdminCourseStatus, rejectionReason: reason } : c
      );
      return delay({ id, decision }, 400);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export function useToggleCourseFlag() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, field }: { id: string; field: "featured" | "pinned" }) => {
      COURSES_STATE = COURSES_STATE.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, [field]: !c[field] } : c));
      return delay({ id, field }, 200);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export function useAdminCategoryList() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories,
    queryFn: async () => delay([...CATEGORIES_STATE])
  });
}

export function useCreateCategory() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { name: string; tags: string[] }) => {
      const slug = input.name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/(^-|-$)/g, "");
      const created: AdminCategory = { id: slug, name: input.name, slug, courseCount: 0, tags: input.tags };
      CATEGORIES_STATE = [...CATEGORIES_STATE, created];
      return delay(created, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories });
    }
  });
}

export function useUpdateCategory() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, name, tags }: { id: string; name: string; tags: string[] }) => {
      CATEGORIES_STATE = CATEGORIES_STATE.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, name, tags } : c));
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories });
    }
  });
}

export function useDeleteCategory() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: string) => {
      CATEGORIES_STATE = CATEGORIES_STATE.filter((c) => c.id !== id);
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories });
    }
  });
}

FILE: packages\api-client\src\hooks\useAdminFinance.ts
