import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  ADMIN_DUMMY_TRANSACTIONS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_PAYOUTS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_REFUNDS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_TAX_RULES,
  type AdminTransaction,
  type AdminPayout,
  type PayoutStatus,
  type AdminRefund,
  type RefundStatus,
  type TaxRule
} from "../dummy-data-admin-finance";

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 350): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: demo-only, in-memory. Replace with real Finance/Payouts/Refunds/Tax API (Doc 3).
let TRANSACTIONS_STATE: AdminTransaction[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_TRANSACTIONS];
let PAYOUTS_STATE: AdminPayout[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_PAYOUTS];
let REFUNDS_STATE: AdminRefund[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_REFUNDS];
let TAX_RULES_STATE: TaxRule[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_TAX_RULES];

export const adminFinanceKeys = {
  all: ["admin", "finance"] as const,
  transactions: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "finance", "transactions", filters] as const,
  payouts: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "finance", "payouts", filters] as const,
  refunds: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "finance", "refunds", filters] as const,
  taxRules: ["admin", "finance", "tax-rules"] as const
};

export type TransactionFilters = {
  search?: string;
  status?: string;
  gateway?: string;
};

export function useAdminTransactions(filters: TransactionFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.transactions(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...TRANSACTIONS_STATE];
      if (filters.search) {
        const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        items = items.filter(
          (t) =>
            t.courseTitle.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            t.learnerName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            t.instructorName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            t.id.toLowerCase().includes(q)
        );
      }
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((t) => t.status === filters.status);
      if (filters.gateway) items = items.filter((t) => t.gateway === filters.gateway);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export type PayoutFilters = {
  status?: string;
};

export function useAdminPayouts(filters: PayoutFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.payouts(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...PAYOUTS_STATE];
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((p) => p.status === filters.status);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useUpdatePayoutStatus() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, status }: { id: string; status: PayoutStatus }) => {
      PAYOUTS_STATE = PAYOUTS_STATE.map((p) =>
        p.id === id
          ? { ...p, status, processedAt: status === "paid" ? new Date().toISOString() : p.processedAt }
          : p
      );
      return delay({ id, status }, 400);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export type RefundFilters = {
  status?: string;
};

export function useAdminRefunds(filters: RefundFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.refunds(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...REFUNDS_STATE];
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((r) => r.status === filters.status);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useResolveRefund() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, status }: { id: string; status: RefundStatus }) => {
      REFUNDS_STATE = REFUNDS_STATE.map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, status } : r));
      if (status === "approved") {
        const refund = REFUNDS_STATE.find((r) => r.id === id);
        if (refund) {
          TRANSACTIONS_STATE = TRANSACTIONS_STATE.map((t) =>
            t.id === refund.transactionId ? { ...t, status: "refunded" as const } : t
          );
        }
      }
      return delay({ id, status }, 400);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export function useAdminTaxRules() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.taxRules,
    queryFn: async () => delay([...TAX_RULES_STATE])
  });
}

export function useUpdateTaxRule() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, ratePercent, active }: { id: string; ratePercent: number; active: boolean }) => {
      TAX_RULES_STATE = TAX_RULES_STATE.map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, ratePercent, active } : r));
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.taxRules });
    }
  });
}

export function useCreateTaxRule() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { region: string; ratePercent: number; appliesTo: string }) => {
      const created: TaxRule = {
        id: `tax_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        region: input.region,
        ratePercent: input.ratePercent,
        appliesTo: input.appliesTo,
        active: true
      };
      TAX_RULES_STATE = [...TAX_RULES_STATE, created];
      return delay(created, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.taxRules });
    }
  });
}

FILE: packages\api-client\src\hooks\useAdminMarketing.ts

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  ADMIN_DUMMY_BANNERS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_COUPONS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_PROMOTION_SALES,
  type AdminBanner,
  type AdminCoupon,
  type PromotionSale
} from "../dummy-data-admin-marketing";

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 350): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: demo-only, in-memory. Replace with real Marketing API (Doc 3) once available.
let BANNERS_STATE: AdminBanner[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_BANNERS];
let COUPONS_STATE: AdminCoupon[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_COUPONS];
let PROMOTION_SALES_STATE: PromotionSale[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_PROMOTION_SALES];

export const adminMarketingKeys = {
  banners: ["admin", "marketing", "banners"] as const,
  coupons: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "marketing", "coupons", filters] as const,
  promotionSales: ["admin", "marketing", "promotion-sales"] as const
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Banners
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function useAdminBanners() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminMarketingKeys.banners,
    queryFn: async () => delay([...BANNERS_STATE].sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order))
  });
}

export function useCreateBanner() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: Omit<AdminBanner, "id" | "order">) => {
      const created: AdminBanner = {
        ...input,
        id: `ban_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        order: BANNERS_STATE.length + 1
      };
      BANNERS_STATE = [...BANNERS_STATE, created];
      return delay(created, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminMarketingKeys.banners });
    }
  });
}

export function useUpdateBanner() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, ...patch }: Partial<AdminBanner> & { id: string }) => {
      BANNERS_STATE = BANNERS_STATE.map((b) => (b.id === id ? { ...b, ...patch } : b));
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminMarketingKeys.banners });
    }
  });
}

export function useDeleteBanner() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: string) => {
      BANNERS_STATE = BANNERS_STATE.filter((b) => b.id !== id);
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminMarketingKeys.banners });
    }
  });
}

export function useReorderBanner() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, direction }: { id: string; direction: "up" | "down" }) => {
      const sorted = [...BANNERS_STATE].sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order);
      const index = sorted.findIndex((b) => b.id === id);
      const swapWith = direction === "up" ? index - 1 : index + 1;
      if (swapWith < 0 || swapWith >= sorted.length) return delay({ id }, 100);
      const a = sorted[index];
      const b = sorted[swapWith];
      const aOrder = a.order;
      a.order = b.order;
      b.order = aOrder;
      BANNERS_STATE = sorted;
      return delay({ id }, 250);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminMarketingKeys.banners });
    }
  });
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Coupons
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export type CouponFilters = {
  search?: string;
  active?: boolean;
};

export function useAdminCoupons(filters: CouponFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminMarketingKeys.coupons(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...COUPONS_STATE];
      if (filters.search) {
        const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        items = items.filter((c) => c.code.toLowerCase().includes(q));
      }
      if (typeof filters.active === "boolean") items = items.filter((c) => c.active === filters.active);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useCreateCoupon() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: Omit<AdminCoupon, "id" | "usageCount">) => {
      const created: AdminCoupon = {
        ...input,
        id: `cpn_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        usageCount: 0
      };
      COUPONS_STATE = [...COUPONS_STATE, created];
      return delay(created, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "marketing", "coupons"] });
    }
  });
}

export function useToggleCouponActive() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: string) => {
      COUPONS_STATE = COUPONS_STATE.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, active: !c.active } : c));
      return delay({ id }, 200);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "marketing", "coupons"] });
    }
  });
}

export function useDeleteCoupon() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: string) => {
      COUPONS_STATE = COUPONS_STATE.filter((c) => c.id !== id);
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "marketing", "coupons"] });
    }
  });
}

  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminMarketingKeys.promotionSales,
    queryFn: async () => delay([...PROMOTION_SALES_STATE])

FILE: packages\api-client\src\hooks\useAdminModeration.ts

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  ADMIN_DUMMY_FLAGGED_REVIEWS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_FLAGGED_FORUM_POSTS,
  type FlaggedReview,
  type FlaggedForumPost,
  type FlagStatus
} from "../dummy-data-admin-mod-support";

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 350): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: demo-only, in-memory. Replace with real Content Moderation API (Doc 3).
let FLAGGED_REVIEWS_STATE: FlaggedReview[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_FLAGGED_REVIEWS];
let FLAGGED_FORUM_STATE: FlaggedForumPost[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_FLAGGED_FORUM_POSTS];

export const adminModerationKeys = {
  reviews: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "moderation", "reviews", filters] as const,
  forum: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "moderation", "forum", filters] as const
};

export type ModerationFilters = {
  status?: string;
};

export function useFlaggedReviews(filters: ModerationFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminModerationKeys.reviews(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...FLAGGED_REVIEWS_STATE];
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((r) => r.status === filters.status);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useResolveFlaggedReview() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, status }: { id: string; status: FlagStatus }) => {
      FLAGGED_REVIEWS_STATE = FLAGGED_REVIEWS_STATE.map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, status } : r));
      return delay({ id, status }, 350);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "moderation", "reviews"] });
    }
  });
}

export function useFlaggedForumPosts(filters: ModerationFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminModerationKeys.forum(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...FLAGGED_FORUM_STATE];
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((p) => p.status === filters.status);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useResolveFlaggedForumPost() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, status }: { id: string; status: FlagStatus }) => {
      FLAGGED_FORUM_STATE = FLAGGED_FORUM_STATE.map((p) => (p.id === id ? { ...p, status } : p));
      return delay({ id, status }, 350);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "moderation", "forum"] });
    }
  });
}

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  ADMIN_DUMMY_TICKETS,
  type SupportTicket,
  type TicketStatus,
  type TicketMessage
} from "../dummy-data-admin-mod-support";

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 350): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: demo-only, in-memory. Replace with real Support/Helpdesk API (Doc 3).
let TICKETS_STATE: SupportTicket[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_TICKETS];

export const adminSupportKeys = {
  list: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "support", "tickets", filters] as const,
  detail: (id: string) => ["admin", "support", "tickets", "detail", id] as const
};

export type TicketFilters = {
  search?: string;
  status?: string;
  priority?: string;
};

export function useAdminTickets(filters: TicketFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminSupportKeys.list(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...TICKETS_STATE];
      if (filters.search) {
        const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        items = items.filter(
          (t) => t.subject.toLowerCase().includes(q) || t.requesterName.toLowerCase().includes(q)
        );
      }
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((t) => t.status === filters.status);
      if (filters.priority) items = items.filter((t) => t.priority === filters.priority);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useAdminTicketDetail(id: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminSupportKeys.detail(id),
    queryFn: async () => {
      const found = TICKETS_STATE.find((t) => t.id === id);
      if (!found) throw new Error("Ticket not found");
      return delay(found);
    },
    enabled: Boolean(id)
  });
}

export function useUpdateTicketStatus() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, status }: { id: string; status: TicketStatus }) => {
      TICKETS_STATE = TICKETS_STATE.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, status } : t));
      return delay({ id, status }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: (_data, variables) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "support", "tickets"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminSupportKeys.detail(variables.id) });
    }
  });
}

export function useAssignTicket() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, assignedTo }: { id: string; assignedTo: string }) => {
      TICKETS_STATE = TICKETS_STATE.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, assignedTo } : t));
      return delay({ id, assignedTo }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: (_data, variables) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "support", "tickets"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminSupportKeys.detail(variables.id) });
    }
  });
}

export function useAddTicketMessage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, body, isInternal }: { id: string; body: string; isInternal: boolean }) => {
      const message: TicketMessage = {
        id: `m_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        authorName: "Admin",
        isInternal,
        isStaff: true,
        body,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
      };
      TICKETS_STATE = TICKETS_STATE.map((t) =>
        t.id === id ? { ...t, messages: [...t.messages, message], status: t.status === "open" ? "in_progress" : t.status } : t
      );
      return delay(message, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: (_data, variables) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "support", "tickets"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminSupportKeys.detail(variables.id) });
    }
  });
}

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 350): Promise<T> {

}

// NOTE: demo-only, in-memory. Replace with real Admin-Users API (Doc 3) once available.

================================================================================
FILE: packages\api-client\src\hooks\useAuth.ts
================================================================================
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { authKeys } from "../query-keys";
import type { Session } from "../types";
import type { LoginInput, RegisterInput } from "@saachi/utils";
import { DUMMY_SESSION } from "../dummy-data";

const SESSION_COOKIE = "saachi_session";
const SESSION_STORAGE_KEY = "saachi_demo_session";

function setSessionCookie() {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  document.cookie = `${SESSION_COOKIE}=demo_token; path=/; max-age=${60 * 60 * 24 * 7}`;
}

function clearSessionCookie() {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  document.cookie = `${SESSION_COOKIE}=; path=/; max-age=0`;
}

function readStoredSession(): Session | null {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
  const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY);
  if (!raw) return null;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(raw) as Session;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function storeSession(session: Session) {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  window.localStorage.setItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(session));
}

function clearStoredSession() {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  window.localStorage.removeItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY);
}

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 500): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: Demo mode. There is no backend yet, so login/register accept ANY
// email/password and create a local mock session. Swap these mutationFns
// for real apiRequest(...) calls once the NestJS backend is available.

export function useSession() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: authKeys.session,
    queryFn: async () => readStoredSession(),
    staleTime: 60 * 1000
  });
}

export function useLogin() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: LoginInput) => {
      const session: Session = {
        ...DUMMY_SESSION,
        email: input.email || DUMMY_SESSION.email
      };
      return delay(session);
    },
    onSuccess: (session) => {
      storeSession(session);
      setSessionCookie();
      queryClient.setQueryData(authKeys.session, session);
    }
  });
}

export function useRegister() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: RegisterInput) => {
      const session: Session = {
        ...DUMMY_SESSION,
        fullName: input.fullName,
        email: input.email,
        role: input.role === "instructor" ? "instructor" : "learner"
      };
      return delay(session);
    },
    onSuccess: (session) => {
      storeSession(session);
      setSessionCookie();
      queryClient.setQueryData(authKeys.session, session);
    }
  });
}

export function useLogout() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      clearStoredSession();
      clearSessionCookie();
      return delay(undefined, 200);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.clear();
    }
  });
}


================================================================================
FILE: packages\api-client\src\hooks\useCourses.ts
================================================================================
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { courseKeys } from "../query-keys";
import type { Course, CourseDetail, Paginated } from "../types";
import { DUMMY_COURSES, type DummyCourse } from "../dummy-data";

export type CourseListFilters = {
  category?: string;
  search?: string;
  priceMin?: number;
  priceMax?: number;
  level?: string;
  language?: string;
  sort?: string;
  page?: number;
};

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 400): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

function toSummary(course: DummyCourse): Course {
  return {
    id: course.id,
    slug: course.slug,
    title: course.title,
    subtitle: course.subtitle,
    thumbnailUrl: course.thumbnailUrl,
    price: course.price,
    currency: course.currency,
    rating: course.rating,
    ratingCount: course.ratingCount,
    instructor: course.instructor,
    status: course.status
  };
}

export function useCourseList(filters: CourseListFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: courseKeys.list(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...DUMMY_COURSES];

      if (filters.search) {
        const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        items = items.filter(
          (c) => c.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.instructor.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(q)
        );
      }
      if (filters.category) {
        items = items.filter((c) => c.categoryId === filters.category);
      }
      if (filters.level) {
        items = items.filter((c) => c.level === filters.level);
      }
      if (filters.language) {
        items = items.filter((c) => c.language === filters.language);
      }
      if (typeof filters.priceMin === "number") {
        items = items.filter((c) => c.price >= (filters.priceMin as number));
      }
      if (typeof filters.priceMax === "number") {
        items = items.filter((c) => c.price <= (filters.priceMax as number));
      }

      switch (filters.sort) {
        case "newest":
          items = [...items].reverse();
          break;
        case "price_asc":
          items = [...items].sort((a, b) => a.price - b.price);
          break;
        case "price_desc":
          items = [...items].sort((a, b) => b.price - a.price);
          break;
        case "rating":
          items = [...items].sort((a, b) => b.rating - a.rating);
          break;
        case "popular":
        default:
          items = [...items].sort((a, b) => b.ratingCount - a.ratingCount);
      }

      const page = filters.page ?? 1;
      const pageSize = 12;
      const start = (page - 1) * pageSize;
      const pageItems = items.slice(start, start + pageSize).map(toSummary);

      return delay<Paginated<Course>>({
        items: pageItems,
        total: items.length,
        page,
        pageSize
      });
    }
  });
}

export function useCourseDetail(idOrSlug: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: courseKeys.detail(idOrSlug),
    queryFn: async () => {
      const found = DUMMY_COURSES.find((c) => c.slug === idOrSlug || c.id === idOrSlug);
      if (!found) throw new Error("Course not found");
      const { categoryId, level, language, ...detail } = found;
      return delay<CourseDetail>(detail);
    },
    enabled: Boolean(idOrSlug)
  });
}

export function useCreateCourse() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: Partial<Course>) => {
      const created: Course = {
        id: `crs_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        slug: (input.slug as string) ?? "new-course",
        title: input.title ?? "Untitled course",
        price: input.price ?? 0,
        currency: input.currency ?? "USD",
        rating: 0,
        ratingCount: 0,
        instructor: input.instructor ?? { id: "ins_demo", fullName: "Demo Instructor" },
        status: "draft"
      };
      return delay(created);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: courseKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export function useUpdateCourse(id: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: Partial<Course>) => {
      const existing = DUMMY_COURSES.find((c) => c.id === id);
      const base: Course = existing
        ? toSummary(existing)
        : {
            id,
            slug: id,
            title: "Untitled",
            price: 0,
            currency: "USD",
            rating: 0,
            ratingCount: 0,
            instructor: { id: "ins_demo", fullName: "Demo Instructor" },
            status: "draft"
          };
      const updated: Course = { ...base, ...input };
      return delay(updated);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: courseKeys.detail(id) });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: courseKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export type UserRole = "learner" | "instructor" | "admin" | "super_admin";

export type Session = {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  role: UserRole;
  avatarUrl?: string;
};

export type Course = {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  price: number;
  currency: string;
  rating: number;
  ratingCount: number;
  instructor: {
    id: string;
    fullName: string;
    avatarUrl?: string;
  };
  status: "draft" | "pending_review" | "published" | "rejected" | "archived";
};

export type CourseLesson = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  type: "video" | "text" | "quiz" | "resource";
  durationSeconds?: number;
  isPreview?: boolean;
};

export type CourseModule = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  lessons: CourseLesson[];
};

export type RatingBreakdown = {
  5: number;
  4: number;
  3: number;
  2: number;
  1: number;
};

export type CourseReview = {
  id: string;
  authorName: string;
  authorAvatarUrl?: string;
  rating: number;
  comment: string;
  createdAt: string;
  verifiedPurchase?: boolean;
};

export type CourseFaqItem = {
  id: string;
  question: string;
  answer: string;
};

export type CourseDetail = Course & {
  description?: string;
  previewVideoUrl?: string;
  instructorBio?: string;
  instructorCourseCount?: number;
  instructorStudentCount?: number;
  curriculum?: CourseModule[];
  ratingBreakdown?: RatingBreakdown;
  reviews?: CourseReview[];
  faq?: CourseFaqItem[];
  relatedCourses?: Course[];
};

export type Paginated<T> = {
  items: T[];
  total: number;
  page: number;
  pageSize: number;
};

export type TransactionStatus = "completed" | "pending" | "failed" | "refunded";

export type AdminTransaction = {
  id: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  learnerName: string;
  instructorName: string;
  amount: number;
  currency: string;
  commission: number;
  netToInstructor: number;
  gateway: "stripe" | "paypal" | "easypaisa" | "jazzcash" | "bank_transfer";
  status: TransactionStatus;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type PayoutStatus = "pending" | "approved" | "paid" | "rejected";

export type AdminPayout = {
  id: string;
  instructorName: string;
  instructorId: string;
  amount: number;
  currency: string;
  method: "bank_transfer" | "easypaisa" | "jazzcash" | "stripe_connect";
  status: PayoutStatus;
  requestedAt: string;
  processedAt?: string;
};

export type RefundStatus = "pending" | "approved" | "rejected";

export type AdminRefund = {
  id: string;
  transactionId: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  learnerName: string;
  amount: number;
  currency: string;
  reason: string;
  status: RefundStatus;
  requestedAt: string;
};

export type TaxRule = {
  id: string;
  region: string;
  ratePercent: number;
  appliesTo: string;
  active: boolean;
};

function daysAgoIso(n: number): string {
  return new Date(Date.now() - n * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
}

export type BannerStatus = "scheduled" | "active" | "expired" | "draft";

export type AdminBanner = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  imageUrl: string;
  linkUrl: string;
  status: BannerStatus;
  startsAt: string;
  endsAt: string;
  order: number;
};

export type CouponType = "percent" | "fixed";

export type AdminCoupon = {
  id: string;
  code: string;
  type: CouponType;
  value: number;
  scope: "platform_wide" | "category" | "course";
  usageLimit: number;
  usageCount: number;
  expiresAt: string;
  active: boolean;
};

export type PromotionTier = "bronze" | "silver" | "gold";

export type PromotionSale = {
  id: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  instructorName: string;
  tier: PromotionTier;
  amount: number;
  currency: string;
  purchasedAt: string;
  expiresAt: string;
};

function img(seed: string): string {
  return `https://picsum.photos/seed/${encodeURIComponent(seed)}/1200/400`;
}

function daysAgoIso(n: number): string {
  return new Date(Date.now() - n * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
}

function daysFromNowIso(n: number): string {
  return new Date(Date.now() + n * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
}

export type FlagStatus = "pending" | "removed" | "dismissed" | "warned";

export type FlaggedReview = {
  id: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  authorName: string;
  comment: string;
  rating: number;
  flagReason: string;
  flaggedBy: string;
  status: FlagStatus;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type FlaggedForumPost = {
  id: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  authorName: string;
  body: string;
  flagReason: string;
  flaggedBy: string;
  status: FlagStatus;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type TicketStatus = "open" | "in_progress" | "resolved" | "closed";
export type TicketPriority = "low" | "medium" | "high" | "urgent";

export type TicketMessage = {
  id: string;
  authorName: string;
  isInternal: boolean;
  isStaff: boolean;
  body: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type SupportTicket = {
  id: string;
  subject: string;
  requesterName: string;
  requesterEmail: string;
  category: string;
  priority: TicketPriority;
  status: TicketStatus;
  assignedTo?: string;
  createdAt: string;
  slaDueAt: string;
  messages: TicketMessage[];
};

function hoursAgoIso(n: number): string {
  return new Date(Date.now() - n * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
}

function hoursFromNowIso(n: number): string {
  return new Date(Date.now() + n * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
}

export type AdminUserRole = "learner" | "instructor" | "corporate" | "admin";
export type AdminUserStatus = "active" | "suspended" | "banned" | "pending";

export type AdminUser = {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  avatarUrl?: string;
  role: AdminUserRole;
  status: AdminUserStatus;
  joinedAt: string;
  lastActiveAt: string;
  coursesEnrolled?: number;
  coursesPublished?: number;
  totalSpend?: number;
  totalEarnings?: number;
  location?: string;
};

export type AdminActivityLogEntry = {
  id: string;
  action: string;
  detail: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type AdminAuditLogEntry = {
  id: string;
  actor: string;
  action: string;
  target: string;
  createdAt: string;
  ip: string;
};

function img(seed: string): string {
  return `https://picsum.photos/seed/${encodeURIComponent(seed)}/150/150`;
}

function daysAgo(n: number): string {
  return new Date(Date.now() - n * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
}

import type { Course, CourseDetail, Session } from "./types";

function img(seed: string, w: number, h: number): string {
  return `https://picsum.photos/seed/${encodeURIComponent(seed)}/${w}/${h}`;
}

export type DummyCourse = CourseDetail & {
  categoryId: string;
  level: "beginner" | "intermediate" | "advanced";
  language: "en" | "ur";
};

function toCourseSummary(course: DummyCourse): Course {
  return {
    id: course.id,
    slug: course.slug,
    title: course.title,
    subtitle: course.subtitle,
    thumbnailUrl: course.thumbnailUrl,
    price: course.price,
    currency: course.currency,
    rating: course.rating,
    ratingCount: course.ratingCount,
    instructor: course.instructor,
    status: course.status
  };
}

export const DUMMY_COURSES: DummyCourse[] = BASE_COURSES.map((course, index) => {
  const others = BASE_COURSES.filter((c) => c.id !== course.id);
  const related = [others[index % others.length], others[(index + 1) % others.length]].filter(Boolean);
  return { ...course, relatedCourses: related.map(toCourseSummary) };
});


================================================================================
FILE: packages\api-client\src\http-client.ts
================================================================================
export type ApiError = {
  code: string;
  message: string;
  fieldErrors?: Record<string, string[]>;
};

export class ApiRequestError extends Error {
  code: string;
  fieldErrors?: Record<string, string[]>;
  status: number;

  constructor(status: number, error: ApiError) {
    super(error.message);
    this.name = "ApiRequestError";
    this.status = status;
    this.code = error.code;
    this.fieldErrors = error.fieldErrors;
  }
}

type RequestOptions = {
  method?: "GET" | "POST" | "PATCH" | "PUT" | "DELETE";
  body?: unknown;
  params?: Record<string, string | number | boolean | undefined>;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
};

function buildUrl(path: string, params?: RequestOptions["params"]): string {
  const base = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "";
  const url = new URL(path.replace(/^\//, ""), base.endsWith("/") ? base : `${base}/`);
  if (params) {
    Object.entries(params).forEach(([key, value]) => {
      if (value !== undefined) url.searchParams.set(key, String(value));
    });
  }
  return url.toString();
}

export async function apiRequest<TResponse>(
  path: string,
  options: RequestOptions = {}
): Promise<TResponse> {
  const { method = "GET", body, params, signal } = options;

  const response = await fetch(buildUrl(path, params), {
    method,
    credentials: "include",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json"
    },
    body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
    signal
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    let errorPayload: ApiError = { code: "unknown_error", message: "Something went wrong" };
    try {
      errorPayload = await response.json();
    } catch {
      // response had no JSON body
    }
    throw new ApiRequestError(response.status, errorPayload);
  }

  if (response.status === 204) return undefined as TResponse;
  return response.json() as Promise<TResponse>;
}

FILE: packages\store\src\useCartStore.ts
================================================================================
import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type CartItem = {
  courseId: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  instructorName: string;
  price: number;
  currency: string;
};

export type CouponState = {
  code: string;
  percentOff: number;
} | null;

// NOTE: demo-only coupon codes, validated client-side. Replace with a real
// Coupons API call once the backend (Doc 3) is available.
const KNOWN_COUPONS: Record<string, number> = {
  SAACHI10: 10,
  SAACHI25: 25
};

type CartState = {
  items: CartItem[];
  coupon: CouponState;
  addItem: (item: CartItem) => void;
  removeItem: (courseId: string) => void;
  clear: () => void;
  applyCoupon: (code: string) => { success: boolean; message: string };
  removeCoupon: () => void;
  isInCart: (courseId: string) => boolean;
  subtotal: () => number;
  discount: () => number;
  total: () => number;
};

export const useCartStore = create<CartState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      items: [],
      coupon: null,

      addItem: (item) => {
        const exists = get().items.some((i) => i.courseId === item.courseId);
        if (exists) return;
        set({ items: [...get().items, item] });
      },

      removeItem: (courseId) => {
        set({ items: get().items.filter((i) => i.courseId !== courseId) });
      },

      clear: () => set({ items: [], coupon: null }),

      applyCoupon: (code) => {
        const normalized = code.trim().toUpperCase();
        const percentOff = KNOWN_COUPONS[normalized];
        if (!percentOff) {
          return { success: false, message: "That coupon code isn't valid." };
        }
        set({ coupon: { code: normalized, percentOff } });
        return { success: true, message: `${percentOff}% discount applied.` };
      },

      removeCoupon: () => set({ coupon: null }),

      isInCart: (courseId) => get().items.some((i) => i.courseId === courseId),

      subtotal: () => get().items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.price, 0),

      discount: () => {
        const coupon = get().coupon;
        if (!coupon) return 0;
        return Math.round(get().subtotal() * (coupon.percentOff / 100) * 100) / 100;
      },

      total: () => Math.max(0, get().subtotal() - get().discount())
    }),
    { name: "saachi-cart" }
  )
);

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type CommunityAnswer = {
  id: string;
  authorName: string;
  isInstructor: boolean;
  body: string;
  createdAt: string;
  upvotes: number;
};

export type CommunityQuestion = {
  id: string;
  courseId: string;
  authorName: string;
  body: string;
  createdAt: string;
  upvotes: number;
  answers: CommunityAnswer[];
};

const SEED_QUESTIONS: CommunityQuestion[] = [
  {
    id: "q_1",
    courseId: "crs_001",
    authorName: "Hina Malik",
    body: "For few-shot prompting, how many examples is usually enough before it stops improving results?",
    createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 60 * 30).toISOString(),
    upvotes: 6,
    answers: [
      {
        id: "a_1",
        authorName: "Sana Rizvi",
        isInstructor: true,
        body: "Usually 3-5 well-chosen examples get you most of the benefit - past that you're often better off improving instructions instead.",
        createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 60 * 20).toISOString(),
        upvotes: 9
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    id: "q_2",
    courseId: "crs_001",
    authorName: "Bilal Ahmed",
    body: "Does the prompt library template work with Claude and Gemini too, or is it ChatGPT-specific?",
    createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 60 * 10).toISOString(),
    upvotes: 2,
    answers: []
  }
];

type CommunityState = {
  questions: CommunityQuestion[];
  addQuestion: (courseId: string, authorName: string, body: string) => void;
  addAnswer: (questionId: string, authorName: string, body: string) => void;
  upvoteQuestion: (questionId: string) => void;
};

function genId(prefix: string) {
  return `${prefix}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`;
}

export const useCommunityStore = create<CommunityState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      questions: SEED_QUESTIONS,

      addQuestion: (courseId, authorName, body) => {
        const newQuestion: CommunityQuestion = {
          id: genId("q"),
          courseId,
          authorName,
          body,
          createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
          upvotes: 0,
          answers: []
        };
        set({ questions: [newQuestion, ...get().questions] });
      },

      addAnswer: (questionId, authorName, body) => {
        set({
          questions: get().questions.map((q) =>
            q.id === questionId
              ? {
                  ...q,
                  answers: [
                    ...q.answers,
                    {
                      id: genId("a"),
                      authorName,
                      isInstructor: false,
                      body,
                      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                      upvotes: 0
                    }
                  ]
                }
              : q
          )
        });
      },

      upvoteQuestion: (questionId) => {
        set({
          questions: get().questions.map((q) =>
            q.id === questionId ? { ...q, upvotes: q.upvotes + 1 } : q
          )
        });
      }
    }),
    { name: "saachi-community" }
  )
);

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type TeamMember = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  status: "invited" | "active";
  assignedCourseIds: string[];
  completedCourseIds: string[];
};

export type CorporateAccount = {
  companyName: string;
  seatsPurchased: number;
  seatsUsed: number;
};

const SEED_TEAM: TeamMember[] = [
  {
    id: "tm_1",
    name: "Zainab Rehman",
    email: "zainab@acmeagency.com",
    status: "active",
    assignedCourseIds: ["crs_001", "crs_002"],
    completedCourseIds: ["crs_001"]
  },
  {
    id: "tm_2",
    name: "Ali Raza",
    email: "ali@acmeagency.com",
    status: "active",
    assignedCourseIds: ["crs_003"],
    completedCourseIds: []
  },
  {
    id: "tm_3",
    name: "Mahnoor Fatima",
    email: "mahnoor@acmeagency.com",
    status: "invited",
    assignedCourseIds: [],
    completedCourseIds: []
  }
];

type CorporateState = {
  account: CorporateAccount;
  team: TeamMember[];
  inviteMember: (name: string, email: string) => void;
  removeMember: (id: string) => void;
  assignCourse: (memberId: string, courseId: string) => void;
  purchaseSeats: (count: number) => void;
};

export const useCorporateStore = create<CorporateState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      account: { companyName: "Acme Agency", seatsPurchased: 10, seatsUsed: 3 },
      team: SEED_TEAM,

      inviteMember: (name, email) => {
        const newMember: TeamMember = {
          id: `tm_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`,
          name,
          email,
          status: "invited",
          assignedCourseIds: [],
          completedCourseIds: []
        };
        set({
          team: [...get().team, newMember],
          account: { ...get().account, seatsUsed: get().account.seatsUsed + 1 }
        });
      },

      removeMember: (id) => {
        set({
          team: get().team.filter((m) => m.id !== id),
          account: { ...get().account, seatsUsed: Math.max(0, get().account.seatsUsed - 1) }
        });
      },

      assignCourse: (memberId, courseId) => {
        set({
          team: get().team.map((m) =>
            m.id === memberId && !m.assignedCourseIds.includes(courseId)
              ? { ...m, assignedCourseIds: [...m.assignedCourseIds, courseId] }
              : m
          )
        });
      },

      purchaseSeats: (count) => {
        set({ account: { ...get().account, seatsPurchased: get().account.seatsPurchased + count } });
      }
    }),
    { name: "saachi-corporate" }
  )
);


================================================================================
FILE: packages\store\src\useEnrollmentStore.ts
================================================================================
import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type EnrollmentRecord = {
  courseId: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  instructorName: string;
  progressPercent: number;
  purchasedAt: string;
  completedLessonIds: string[];
};

type EnrollmentState = {
  enrollments: EnrollmentRecord[];
  isEnrolled: (courseId: string) => boolean;
  enroll: (records: Omit<EnrollmentRecord, "progressPercent" | "purchasedAt" | "completedLessonIds">[]) => void;
  markLessonComplete: (courseId: string, lessonId: string, totalLessons: number) => void;
  getEnrollment: (courseId: string) => EnrollmentRecord | undefined;
};

export const useEnrollmentStore = create<EnrollmentState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      enrollments: [],

      isEnrolled: (courseId) => get().enrollments.some((e) => e.courseId === courseId),

      getEnrollment: (courseId) => get().enrollments.find((e) => e.courseId === courseId),

      enroll: (records) => {
        const now = new Date().toISOString();
        const existingIds = new Set(get().enrollments.map((e) => e.courseId));
        const fresh = records
          .filter((r) => !existingIds.has(r.courseId))
          .map((r) => ({ ...r, progressPercent: 0, purchasedAt: now, completedLessonIds: [] as string[] }));
        if (fresh.length === 0) return;
        set({ enrollments: [...get().enrollments, ...fresh] });
      },

      markLessonComplete: (courseId, lessonId, totalLessons) => {
        set({
          enrollments: get().enrollments.map((e) => {
            if (e.courseId !== courseId) return e;
            const completedLessonIds = e.completedLessonIds.includes(lessonId)
              ? e.completedLessonIds
              : [...e.completedLessonIds, lessonId];
            const progressPercent =
              totalLessons > 0 ? Math.round((completedLessonIds.length / totalLessons) * 100) : 0;
            return { ...e, completedLessonIds, progressPercent };
          })
        });
      }
    }),
    { name: "saachi-enrollments" }
  )
);

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type InstructorCourseLesson = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  type: "video" | "text" | "quiz" | "resource";
};

export type InstructorCourseModule = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  lessons: InstructorCourseLesson[];
};

export type InstructorCourse = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  categoryId: string;
  language: string;
  level: "beginner" | "intermediate" | "advanced";
  price: number;
  currency: string;
  status: "draft" | "pending_review" | "published";
  modules: InstructorCourseModule[];
  createdAt: string;
  enrollmentCount: number;
  revenue: number;
  rating: number;
};

type InstructorCourseState = {
  courses: InstructorCourse[];
  addCourse: (course: InstructorCourse) => void;
  updateCourse: (id: string, patch: Partial<InstructorCourse>) => void;
  removeCourse: (id: string) => void;
};

export const useInstructorCourseStore = create<InstructorCourseState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      courses: [],
      addCourse: (course) => set({ courses: [course, ...get().courses] }),
      updateCourse: (id, patch) =>
        set({ courses: get().courses.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, ...patch } : c)) }),
      removeCourse: (id) => set({ courses: get().courses.filter((c) => c.id !== id) })
    }),
    { name: "saachi-instructor-courses" }
  )
);

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type ActivePromotion = {
  courseId: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  tier: "bronze" | "silver" | "gold";
  purchasedAt: string;
  expiresAt: string;
};

type PromotionState = {
  active: ActivePromotion[];
  purchase: (courseId: string, courseTitle: string, tier: ActivePromotion["tier"]) => void;
};

export const usePromotionStore = create<PromotionState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      active: [],
      purchase: (courseId, courseTitle, tier) => {
        const now = new Date();
        const expires = new Date(now.getTime() + 1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 14);
        set({
          active: [
            ...get().active.filter((p) => p.courseId !== courseId),
            { courseId, courseTitle, tier, purchasedAt: now.toISOString(), expiresAt: expires.toISOString() }
          ]
        });
      }
    }),
    { name: "saachi-promotions" }
  )
);

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type WishlistItem = {
  courseId: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  instructorName: string;
  price: number;
  currency: string;
  rating: number;
  ratingCount: number;
};

type WishlistState = {
  items: WishlistItem[];
  toggle: (item: WishlistItem) => void;
  remove: (courseId: string) => void;
  isWishlisted: (courseId: string) => boolean;
};

export const useWishlistStore = create<WishlistState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      items: [],

      toggle: (item) => {
        const exists = get().items.some((i) => i.courseId === item.courseId);
        if (exists) {
          set({ items: get().items.filter((i) => i.courseId !== item.courseId) });
        } else {
          set({ items: [...get().items, item] });
        }
      },

      remove: (courseId) => {
        set({ items: get().items.filter((i) => i.courseId !== courseId) });
      },

      isWishlisted: (courseId) => get().items.some((i) => i.courseId === courseId)
    }),
    { name: "saachi-wishlist" }
  )
);

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\Accordion.tsx

"use client";

import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type AccordionItemData = {
  id: string;
  title: ReactNode;
  content: ReactNode;
  meta?: ReactNode;
};

export type AccordionProps = {
  items: AccordionItemData[];
  defaultOpenId?: string;
  className?: string;
};

export function Accordion({ items, defaultOpenId, className }: AccordionProps) {
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState<string | null>(defaultOpenId ?? null);

  return (
    <div className={cn("flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line divide-y divide-line", className)}>
      {items.map((item) => {
        const isOpen = openId === item.id;
        return (
          <div key={item.id} className="bg-card">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setOpenId(isOpen ? null : item.id)}
              className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-4 px-5 py-4 text-left"
              aria-expanded={isOpen}
            >
              <div className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-3">
                <span className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{item.title}</span>
                {item.meta}
              </div>
              <ChevronDown
                size={18}
                className={cn(
                  "shrink-0 text-ink-soft transition-transform duration-200 ease-out",
                  isOpen && "rotate-180 text-gold-dark"
                )}
              />
            </button>
            <div
              className={cn(
                "grid transition-all duration-200 ease-out",
                isOpen ? "grid-rows-[1fr] opacity-100" : "grid-rows-[0fr] opacity-0"
              )}
            >
              <div className="overflow-hidden">
                <div className="px-5 pb-4 text-[14px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft">{item.content}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\Badge.tsx

import type { HTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

type BadgeVariant = "default" | "success" | "warning" | "error" | "gold";

export type BadgeProps = HTMLAttributes<HTMLSpanElement> & {
  variant?: BadgeVariant;
};

const variantStyles: Record<BadgeVariant, string> = {
  default: "bg-paper-dim text-ink-soft",
  success: "bg-teal-light text-teal",
  warning: "bg-gold-tint text-gold-dark",
  error: "bg-error-tint text-error",
  gold: "bg-gold text-ink"
};

export function Badge({ className, variant = "default", children, ...props }: BadgeProps) {
  return (
    <span
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold",
        variantStyles[variant],
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\Button.tsx
================================================================================
import { forwardRef, type ButtonHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

type ButtonVariant = "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "danger";
type ButtonSize = "sm" | "md" | "lg";

export type ButtonProps = ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: ButtonSize;
  loading?: boolean;
};

const variantStyles: Record<ButtonVariant, string> = {
  primary: "bg-gold text-ink hover:bg-gold-dark",
  secondary: "border border-line-strong bg-card text-ink hover:border-ink",
  ghost: "bg-transparent text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim",
  danger: "bg-error text-white hover:bg-[#a53931]"
};

const sizeStyles: Record<ButtonSize, string> = {
  sm: "h-9 px-4 text-sm",
  md: "h-11 px-5 text-[15px]",
  lg: "h-[52px] px-7 text-[16px]"
};

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant = "primary", size = "md", loading, disabled, children, ...props }, ref) => {
    return (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        disabled={disabled || loading}
        className={cn(
          "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full font-semibold tracking-[-0.01em]",
          "transition-colors duration-150 ease-out disabled:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none",
          variantStyles[variant],
          sizeStyles[size],
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {loading ? "Workingâ€¦" : children}
      </button>
    );
  }
);
Button.displayName = "Button";

import { forwardRef, type InputHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { Check } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type CheckboxProps = Omit<InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>, "type"> & {
  label?: string;
};

export const Checkbox = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, CheckboxProps>(
  ({ className, label, id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const checkboxId = id ?? props.name;

    return (
      <label htmlFor={checkboxId} className="inline-flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2.5 select-none">
        <span className="relative inline-flex h-[18px] w-[18px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center">
          <input
            ref={ref}
            type="checkbox"
            id={checkboxId}
            className={cn("peer absolute inset-0 h-full w-full cursor-pointer opacity-0", className)}
            {...props}
          />
          <span className="flex h-[18px] w-[18px] items-center justify-center rounded-[5px] border border-line-strong bg-card transition-colors duration-150 ease-out peer-checked:border-gold peer-checked:bg-gold peer-focus-visible:ring-2 peer-focus-visible:ring-gold/40">
            <Check size={13} strokeWidth={3} className="text-ink opacity-0 peer-checked:opacity-100" />
          </span>
        </span>
        {label && <span className="text-[14px] text-ink">{label}</span>}
      </label>
    );
  }
);
Checkbox.displayName = "Checkbox";

import Link from "next/link";
import { Star } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type CourseCardProps = {
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  instructorName: string;
  price: number;
  currency?: string;
  rating: number;
  ratingCount: number;
  className?: string;
};

export function CourseCard({
  slug,
  title,
  thumbnailUrl,
  instructorName,
  price,
  currency = "USD",
  rating,
  ratingCount,
  className
}: CourseCardProps) {
  const formattedPrice = new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
    minimumFractionDigits: price % 1 === 0 ? 0 : 2
  }).format(price);

  return (
    <Link
      href={`/courses/${slug}`}
      className={cn(
        "group flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card",
        "transition-all duration-200 ease-out hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-gold-dark/40",
        className
      )}
    >
      <div className="relative aspect-video w-full overflow-hidden bg-paper-dim">
        {thumbnailUrl ? (
          // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
          <img
            src={thumbnailUrl}
            alt={title}
            className="h-full w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 ease-out group-hover:scale-[1.02]"
          />
        ) : (
          <div className="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center text-[13px] text-ink-soft/60">
            No preview
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-2 p-4">
        <h3 className="line-clamp-2 text-[15px] font-semibold leading-snug text-ink">{title}</h3>
        <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{instructorName}</p>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[13px]">
          <Star size={14} className="fill-gold text-gold" />
          <span className="font-semibold text-ink">{rating.toFixed(1)}</span>
          <span className="text-ink-soft/60">({ratingCount.toLocaleString()})</span>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-auto pt-2 font-mono text-[17px] font-semibold text-ink">{formattedPrice}</div>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\DataTable.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { ChevronUp, ChevronDown, ChevronsUpDown } from "lucide-react";
import {
  flexRender,
  getCoreRowModel,
  getPaginationRowModel,
  getSortedRowModel,
  useReactTable,
  type ColumnDef,
  type SortingState
} from "@tanstack/react-table";
import { useState } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";
import { ShimmerTable } from "./Shimmer";
import { EmptyState } from "./EmptyState";
import { Button } from "./Button";

export type DataTableProps<T> = {
  columns: ColumnDef<T, any>[];
  data: T[];
  isLoading?: boolean;
  emptyTitle?: string;
  emptyDescription?: string;
  pageSize?: number;
  toolbar?: ReactNode;
  onRowClick?: (row: T) => void;
};

export function DataTable<T>({
  columns,
  data,
  isLoading,
  emptyTitle = "Nothing here yet",
  emptyDescription,
  pageSize = 10,
  toolbar,
  onRowClick
}: DataTableProps<T>) {
  const [sorting, setSorting] = useState<SortingState>([]);

  const table = useReactTable({
    data,
    columns,
    state: { sorting },
    onSortingChange: setSorting,
    getCoreRowModel: getCoreRowModel(),
    getSortedRowModel: getSortedRowModel(),
    getPaginationRowModel: getPaginationRowModel(),
    initialState: { pagination: { pageSize } }
  });

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        {toolbar}
        <ShimmerTable rows={6} columns={columns.length} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (data.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        {toolbar}
        <EmptyState title={emptyTitle} description={emptyDescription} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      {toolbar}
      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-[13px]">
            <thead className="border-b border-line bg-paper-dim">
              {table.getHeaderGroups().map((headerGroup) => (
                <tr key={headerGroup.id}>
                  {headerGroup.headers.map((header) => {
                    const sortState = header.column.getIsSorted();
                    return (
                      <th key={header.id} className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">
                        {header.isPlaceholder ? null : (
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={header.column.getToggleSortingHandler()}
                            className={cn(
                              "flex items-center gap-1.5",
                              header.column.getCanSort() ? "cursor-pointer select-none hover:text-ink" : "cursor-default"
                            )}
                          >
                            {flexRender(header.column.columnDef.header, header.getContext())}
                            {header.column.getCanSort() &&
                              (sortState === "asc" ? (
                                <ChevronUp size={13} />
                              ) : sortState === "desc" ? (
                                <ChevronDown size={13} />
                              ) : (
                                <ChevronsUpDown size={13} className="opacity-40" />
                              ))}
                          </button>
                        )}
                      </th>
                    );
                  })}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {table.getRowModel().rows.map((row) => (
                <tr
                  key={row.id}
                  onClick={() => onRowClick?.(row.original)}
                  className={cn(
                    "border-b border-line last:border-0",
                    onRowClick && "cursor-pointer transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim"
                  )}
                >
                  {row.getVisibleCells().map((cell) => (
                    <td key={cell.id} className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3.5 text-ink">
                      {flexRender(cell.column.columnDef.cell, cell.getContext())}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {table.getPageCount() > 1 && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-1">
          <span className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
            Page {table.getState().pagination.pageIndex + 1} of {table.getPageCount()}
          </span>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => table.previousPage()} disabled={!table.getCanPreviousPage()}>
              Previous
            </Button>
            <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => table.nextPage()} disabled={!table.getCanNextPage()}>
              Next
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\FilterSidebar.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { Checkbox } from "./Checkbox";
import { Select } from "./Select";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type FilterSidebarValue = {
  categories: string[];
  level?: string;
  language?: string;
};

export type FilterSidebarProps = {
  categoryOptions: { label: string; value: string }[];
  levelOptions: { label: string; value: string }[];
  languageOptions: { label: string; value: string }[];
  value: FilterSidebarValue;
  onChange: (next: FilterSidebarValue) => void;
  className?: string;
};

export function FilterSidebar({
  categoryOptions,
  levelOptions,
  languageOptions,
  value,
  onChange,
  className
}: FilterSidebarProps) {
  const toggleCategory = (cat: string) => {
    const isActive = value.categories.includes(cat);
    const nextCategories = isActive
      ? value.categories.filter((c) => c !== cat)
      : [...value.categories, cat];
    onChange({ ...value, categories: nextCategories });
  };

  return (
    <aside className={cn("flex w-full flex-col gap-8 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6", className)}>
      <div>
        <h4 className="mb-3 text-[13px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-ink-soft/70">
          Category
        </h4>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5">
          {categoryOptions.map((opt) => (
            <Checkbox
              key={opt.value}
              label={opt.label}
              checked={value.categories.includes(opt.value)}
              onChange={() => toggleCategory(opt.value)}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <Select
        label="Skill level"
        placeholder="All levels"
        options={levelOptions}
        value={value.level ?? ""}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, level: e.target.value || undefined })}
      />

      <Select
        label="Language"
        placeholder="All languages"
        options={languageOptions}
        value={value.language ?? ""}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, language: e.target.value || undefined })}
      />

      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => onChange({ categories: [], level: undefined, language: undefined })}
        className="text-left text-[13px] font-medium text-gold-dark hover:underline"
      >
        Clear all filters
      </button>
    </aside>
  );
}

import { forwardRef, type InputHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type InputProps = InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> & {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
  helperText?: string;
};

export const Input = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, InputProps>(
  ({ className, label, error, helperText, id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const inputId = id ?? props.name;

    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        {label && (
          <label htmlFor={inputId} className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">
            {label}
          </label>
        )}
        <input
          ref={ref}
          id={inputId}
          className={cn(
            "h-11 w-full rounded-control border border-line-strong bg-card px-3.5 text-[15px] text-ink",
            "placeholder:text-ink-soft/50 outline-none transition-colors duration-150 ease-out",
            "focus:border-gold",
            error && "border-error focus:border-error",
            className
          )}
          aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}
          {...props}
        />
        {error ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-error">{error}</p>
        ) : helperText ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{helperText}</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  }
);
Input.displayName = "Input";

    <div className={cn("rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5", className)}>

            {delta.value}

  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\Modal.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { type ReactNode, useEffect } from "react";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type ModalProps = {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
  footer?: ReactNode;
  size?: "md" | "lg";
};

export function Modal({ open, onClose, title, children, footer, size = "md" }: ModalProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, [open, onClose]);

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-ink/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div
        className={cn(
          "relative w-full rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6",
          size === "lg" ? "max-w-[720px]" : "max-w-[520px]"
        )}
      >
        {title && (
          <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h2 className="font-display text-[20px] font-semibold text-ink">{title}</h2>
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              aria-label="Close"
              className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-ink-soft transition-colors hover:bg-paper-dim"
            >
              <X size={18} />
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
        <div>{children}</div>
        {footer && <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-3">{footer}</div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { Star, BadgeCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type ReviewCardProps = {
  authorName: string;
  authorAvatarUrl?: string;
  rating: number;
  comment: string;
  createdAt: string;
  verifiedPurchase?: boolean;
  className?: string;
};

export function ReviewCard({
  authorName,
  authorAvatarUrl,
  rating,
  comment,
  createdAt,
  verifiedPurchase,
  className
}: ReviewCardProps) {
  const formattedDate = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    year: "numeric",
    month: "short",
    day: "numeric"
  }).format(new Date(createdAt));

  return (
    <div className={cn("rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6", className)}>
      <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-1">
        {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
          <Star key={i} size={15} className={i < Math.round(rating) ? "fill-gold text-gold" : "text-line-strong"} />
        ))}
      </div>

      <p className="mb-4 text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-ink">{comment}</p>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-teal-light text-[13px] font-semibold text-teal">
          {authorAvatarUrl ? (
            // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
            <img src={authorAvatarUrl} alt={authorName} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
          ) : (
            authorName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()
          )}
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">{authorName}</span>
            {verifiedPurchase && <BadgeCheck size={14} className="text-teal" />}
          </div>
          <span className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft/60">{formattedDate}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { forwardRef, type SelectHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type SelectOption = {
  label: string;
  value: string;
};

export type SelectProps = SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement> & {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
  helperText?: string;
  options: SelectOption[];
  placeholder?: string;
};

export const Select = forwardRef<HTMLSelectElement, SelectProps>(
  ({ className, label, error, helperText, options, placeholder, id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const selectId = id ?? props.name;

    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        {label && (
          <label htmlFor={selectId} className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">
            {label}
          </label>
        )}
        <div className="relative">
          <select
            ref={ref}
            id={selectId}
            className={cn(
              "h-11 w-full appearance-none rounded-control border border-line-strong bg-card px-3.5 pr-10 text-[15px] text-ink",
              "outline-none transition-colors duration-150 ease-out focus:border-gold",
              error && "border-error focus:border-error",
              className
            )}
            aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}
            {...props}
          >
            {placeholder && (
              <option value="" disabled>
                {placeholder}
              </option>
            )}
            {options.map((opt) => (
              <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>
                {opt.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <ChevronDown
            size={18}
            className="pointer-events-none absolute right-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft"
          />
        </div>
        {error ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-error">{error}</p>
        ) : helperText ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{helperText}</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  }
);
Select.displayName = "Select";

================================================================================
import type { HTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type ShimmerProps = HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>;

/** Base shimmer block - grayscale sweep, works in light + dark theme. */
export function Shimmer({ className, ...props }: ShimmerProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "relative overflow-hidden rounded-control bg-paper-dim",
        "before:absolute before:inset-0 before:-translate-x-full",
        "before:bg-gradient-to-r before:from-transparent before:via-white/60 before:to-transparent",
        "before:animate-shimmer dark:before:via-white/10",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export function ShimmerText({ lines = 3, lastLineWidth = "60%" }: { lines?: number; lastLineWidth?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      {Array.from({ length: lines }).map((_, i) => (
        <Shimmer key={i} className="h-3.5" style={{ width: i === lines - 1 ? lastLineWidth : "100%" }} />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ShimmerKpiCard() {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5">
      <Shimmer className="mb-4 h-9 w-9 rounded-full" />
      <Shimmer className="mb-2 h-6 w-20" />
      <Shimmer className="h-3.5 w-28" />
    </div>
  );
}

export function ShimmerTableRow({ columns = 5 }: { columns?: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 border-b border-line px-4 py-3.5">
      {Array.from({ length: columns }).map((_, i) => (
        <Shimmer key={i} className="h-3.5 flex-1" />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ShimmerTable({ rows = 6, columns = 5 }: { rows?: number; columns?: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
      {Array.from({ length: rows }).map((_, i) => (
        <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={columns} />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ShimmerChart() {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
      <Shimmer className="mb-6 h-4 w-32" />
      <Shimmer className="h-[220px] w-full" />
    </div>
  );
}

import type { HTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type SkeletonProps = HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>;

export function Skeleton({ className, ...props }: SkeletonProps) {
  return <div className={cn("animate-pulse rounded-control bg-paper-dim", className)} {...props} />;
}

export function SkeletonText({ lines = 3 }: { lines?: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      {Array.from({ length: lines }).map((_, i) => (
        <Skeleton key={i} className="h-4" style={{ width: i === lines - 1 ? "60%" : "100%" }} />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function SkeletonCard() {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-card border border-line p-6">
      <Skeleton className="mb-4 h-40 w-full" />
      <Skeleton className="mb-2 h-5 w-3/4" />
      <SkeletonText lines={2} />
    </div>
  );
}

import { cn } from "../cn";

type StatusTone = "neutral" | "success" | "warning" | "error" | "info";

export type StatusPillProps = {
  label: string;
  tone?: StatusTone;
  className?: string;
};

const toneStyles: Record<StatusTone, string> = {
  neutral: "bg-paper-dim text-ink-soft border-line",
  success: "bg-success-tint text-success border-success/20",
  warning: "bg-warning-tint text-warning border-warning/20",
  error: "bg-error-tint text-error border-error/20",
  info: "bg-info-tint text-info border-info/20"
};

export function StatusPill({ label, tone = "neutral", className }: StatusPillProps) {
  return (
    <span
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] font-semibold capitalize",
        toneStyles[tone],
        className
      )}
    >
      <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-current" />
      {label}
    </span>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\Textarea.tsx
================================================================================
import { forwardRef, type TextareaHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type TextareaProps = TextareaHTMLAttributes<HTMLTextAreaElement> & {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
  helperText?: string;
};

export const Textarea = forwardRef<HTMLTextAreaElement, TextareaProps>(
  ({ className, label, error, helperText, id, rows = 4, ...props }, ref) => {
    const textareaId = id ?? props.name;

    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        {label && (
          <label htmlFor={textareaId} className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">
            {label}
          </label>
        )}
        <textarea
          ref={ref}
          id={textareaId}
          rows={rows}
          className={cn(
            "w-full resize-y rounded-control border border-line-strong bg-card px-3.5 py-3 text-[15px] leading-[1.55] text-ink",
            "placeholder:text-ink-soft/50 outline-none transition-colors duration-150 ease-out focus:border-gold",
            error && "border-error focus:border-error",
            className
          )}
          aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}
          {...props}
        />
        {error ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-error">{error}</p>
        ) : helperText ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{helperText}</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  }
);
Textarea.displayName = "Textarea";


================================================================================
FILE: packages\ui\src\components\ThemeToggle.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Sun, Moon } from "lucide-react";
import { useThemeStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export function ThemeToggle({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const { theme, toggleTheme } = useThemeStore();
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => setMounted(true), []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return;
    document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);
  }, [theme, mounted]);

  if (!mounted) {
    return <div className={cn("h-9 w-9 shrink-0 rounded-control", className)} />;
  }

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={toggleTheme}
      aria-label={theme === "light" ? "Switch to dark mode" : "Switch to light mode"}
      className={cn(
        "flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim",
        className
      )}
    >
      {theme === "light" ? <Moon size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} /> : <Sun size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />}
    </button>
  );
}
